+/// Exhaustion is observable only when the authored read rule itself contains
+/// a top-level absence check on an exact required point keyed by this row's
+/// intrinsic id. The lane stays private and evaluates the complete rule.
+fn vacancy_branch(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
+    if lane.source.is_some() || lane.scan.filter.is_some() || !lane.scan.prefix.is_empty() { return false; }
+    let Some(outer) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
+    if outer.sync.is_some() { return false; }
+    outer.rules.read.disjuncts().iter().any(|rule| {
+        let RuleExpr::Not(inner) = rule else { return false };
+        let RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } = inner.as_ref() else { return false };
+        let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
+        let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
+        equalities.is_empty() && key == &[RuleTerm::Field("id".into())]
+            && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.len() == 1
+            && point.components[0].column().is_some_and(|column|
+                target.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
+    })
+}
+
 /// An intrinsic-id membership lane retains id as its logical merge position
 /// even though the physical byId scan pins that sole component.
 pub fn membership_point_id(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
@@ -753,7 +773,40 @@ fn membership_witness(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane, null_distinc
             || !equalities.iter().all(|(column, term)| matches(column, term)) { return false; }
         !null_distinct || probe_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().is_some_and(|column| column != source_column
             && fixed(column).is_some_and(|expr| matches!(expr, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) || fixed_non_null_literal(expr))))
-    }, &|rule| rule_true(rule, &lane.scan.table, outer_table, &pinned))
+    }, &|rule| rule_true(rule, &lane.scan.table, outer_table, &pinned)
+        || lane.scan.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, filter, rule, &pins)))
+}
+
+/// A generated membership lane may retain a second, prefix-fixed point grant
+/// as a residual predicate.  Recognize only the exact bound point expression:
+/// this proves that every row emitted by the lane satisfies that conjunct,
+/// while a missing, reordered or merely similar probe keeps the merge out.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — one fixed grant may guard one varying per-row grant.
+fn fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema: &Schema, filter: &Expr, rule: &RuleExpr,
+    pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
+    if let Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::And, left, right } = filter {
+        return fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, left, rule, pins)
+            || fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, right, rule, pins);
+    }
+    let (Expr::Exists(read), RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. }) = (filter, rule) else { return false };
+    let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
+    let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
+    equalities.is_empty() && target.through.is_none() && target.not_before.is_none() && !target.is_expiring()
+        && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.iter().all(|part| part.column()
+            .and_then(|column| target.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
+        && schema.rule_input_is_readable(table, index, key)
+        && &read.table == table && &read.index == index && read.key.len() == key.len()
+        && read.key.iter().zip(key).all(|(expression, term)| rule_term_expression(term, expression, pins))
+}
+
+fn rule_term_expression(term: &RuleTerm, expression: &Expr, pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
+    match (term, expression) {
+        (RuleTerm::Viewer, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) => true,
+        (RuleTerm::Literal(value), Expr::Literal(actual)) => value == actual,
+        (RuleTerm::Field(field), expression) => pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)|
+            (name.as_str() == field).then_some(*value)).is_some_and(|value| value == expression),
+        _ => false,
+    }
 }

+/// A top-level `not exists point[.id]` explicitly makes vacancy observable.
+/// One server lane checks the original rule for every candidate and may report
+/// exhaustion only after the physical range ends. It carries no ordinary
+/// filter because rule probes use wider system visibility.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — exact vacancy is the sole non-uniform exhaustion lane.
+pub(super) fn vacancy(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests,
+    read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {
+    let range = read.range.as_ref()?;
+    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
+    let rule = branches::read_rule(table)?;
+    if read.filter.is_some() || read.index.is_some() || !read.keys.is_empty() || read.matching.is_some()
+        || !read.computed.is_empty() || read.cursor_prefix.is_some()
+        || (table.decl.sync.is_some() && !table.decl.online_only) { return None; }
+    fn alternatives<'a>(value: &'a Expr, out: &mut Vec<&'a Expr>) {
+        match value {
+            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => { alternatives(left, out); alternatives(right, out); }
+            other => out.push(other),
+        }
+    }
+    let mut disjuncts = Vec::new(); alternatives(rule, &mut disjuncts);
+    let admitted = disjuncts.iter().any(|value| {
+        let Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } = value else { return false };
+        let Expr::Exists { read: probe, .. } = value.as_ref() else { return false };
+        if op != "not" || probe.range.is_some() || probe.filter.is_some() || probe.matching.is_some()
+            || !probe.seeks.is_empty() || probe.keys.len() != 1 || intent::column(&probe.keys[0].value) != Some("id") { return false; }
+        let Some(target) = tables.get(&probe.table) else { return false };
+        let Some(columns) = super::intent_lanes::probe_columns(tables, synthesized, table, probe) else { return false };
+        target.indexes.iter().any(|index| target.total_unique(index) && index.columns == columns)
+    });
+    if !admitted { return None; }
+    let mut candidate = requests.clone(); let errors = candidate.error_count();
+    let mut system = read.clone(); candidate.read(tables, &mut system, false);
+    let mut lane = read.clone(); candidate.plan_lane(tables, &mut lane);
+    let mut lanes = Vec::with_capacity(2); let mut order = None;
+    branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, system, SYSTEM_LANE, None, None, None)?;
+    branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, lane, VACANCY_LANE, None, None, None)?;
+    if candidate.error_count() != errors { return None; }
+    *requests = candidate;
+    Some(MergeExpr { view_order: Vec::new(), table: read.table.clone(), matching: None, filter: None,
+        index: String::new(), order: range.order, take: range.take, cursor: range.cursor.clone(), lanes, site: read.site.clone() })
+}

+/// An authored equality may already be the right authorization scope while a
+/// named order index leaves it as a residual filter. Print the two exact edits
+/// together: the compatible declaration and the same query selecting it.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — equality-filter advice is mechanically coherent even when it remains advisory.
+fn equality_filter_index_rewrite(table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table, scan: &snapback4_core::ir::Scan,
+    unpinned: &[String], leading: &[String], declaration: Option<&str>) -> Option<String> {
+    use snapback4_core::ir::{BinaryOp, Expr};
+    fn pins(expr: &Expr, column: &str) -> bool {
+        match expr {
+            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left, right } => pins(left, column) || pins(right, column),
+            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } => field(left, column) && independent(right)
+                || field(right, column) && independent(left),
+            _ => false,
+        }
+    }
+    fn field(expr: &Expr, column: &str) -> bool {
+        matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name } if name == column
+            && matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == "$filter"))
+    }
+    fn independent(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
+        match expr {
+            Expr::Field { base, .. } => !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == "$filter") && independent(base),
+            Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => independent(left) && independent(right),
+            Expr::Unary { value, .. } => independent(value),
+            Expr::Array(values) => values.iter().all(independent),
+            Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().all(independent),
+            Expr::Builtin { args, .. } => args.iter().all(independent),
+            Expr::Get(_) | Expr::Exists(_) | Expr::Scan(_) | Expr::Merge(_) | Expr::Block { .. } => false,
+            _ => true,
+        }
+    }
+    let filter = scan.filter.as_deref()?;
+    if unpinned.is_empty() || leading.len() > 8 || !unpinned.iter().all(|column| pins(filter, column)) { return None; }
+    let title = |value: &str| { let mut chars = value.chars(); chars.next()
+        .map(|first| first.to_uppercase().collect::<String>() + chars.as_str()).unwrap_or_default() };
+    let stem = leading.iter().filter(|column| column.as_str() != "id").map(|column| title(column)).collect::<String>();
+    if stem.is_empty() { return None; }
+    let mut name = format!("by{stem}");
+    let mut suffix = 2;
+    while table.indexes.contains_key(&name) {
+        name = format!("by{stem}{suffix}"); suffix += 1;
+    }
+    let declaration = declaration?;
+    let old = format!(" by {}", scan.index);
+    if !declaration.contains(&old) { return None; }
+    let rewritten = declaration.replacen(&old, &format!(" by {name}"), 1);
+    Some(format!("on table {} add `by {name}: {}`; use the same index in the rewritten query:\n```quarry\n{rewritten}\n```", scan.table, leading.join(", ")))
+}

     if !scan.prefix.iter().any(|key| matches!(key, IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(_))) { return None; }

index 198e9455c7..c33f192dd7 100644
--- a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/lanes.rs

@@ -75,12 +75,24 @@ fn range_width(domain: &A) -> Option<u64> {
 // A lane-dependent predicate needs the eager binding snapshot. Plain row
 // predicates can keep the same head strategy as explicitly written lanes.
 pub(super) fn filter_uses_lane(value: &A, name: &str) -> bool {
+    let read_uses_lane = |read: &ast::ReadExpr| {
+        read.keys.iter().any(|key| filter_uses_lane(&key.value, name))
+            || read.seeks.iter().any(|seek| filter_uses_lane(&seek.value, name))
+            || read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| filter_uses_lane(filter, name))
+            || read.matching.as_deref().is_some_and(|matching| filter_uses_lane(matching, name))
+            || read.range.as_ref().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_deref())
+                .is_some_and(|cursor| filter_uses_lane(cursor, name))
+            || read.computed.values().any(|value| filter_uses_lane(value, name))
+    };
     match value {
         A::Name { name: used, .. } => used == name,
         A::Focal { .. } | A::Literal { .. } => false,
         A::Member { base, .. } | A::Unary { value: base, .. } => filter_uses_lane(base, name),
         A::Binary { left, right, .. } => filter_uses_lane(left, name) || filter_uses_lane(right, name),
         A::Call { args, .. } => args.iter().any(|arg| filter_uses_lane(arg, name)),
+        // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — an independent fixed point guard does
+        // not need a snapshot of the varying membership row.
+        A::Exists { read, .. } | A::Created { read, .. } | A::Read(read) => read_uses_lane(read),
         _ => true,
     }
 }

+#[test]
+fn two_principal_status_scope_and_fixed_plus_per_row_grants_are_complete() {
+    let escorts = "table roles:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  read <- .principal = viewer\n  online only\ntable escorts:\n  student: principal\n  volunteerOne: principal ?\n  volunteerTwo: principal ?\n  status: enum('requested', 'assigned', 'arrived', 'walking', 'completed')\n  at: int\n  read <- .student = viewer or exists roles[viewer] or ((.volunteerOne = viewer or .volunteerTwo = viewer) and (.status = 'assigned' or .status = 'arrived' or .status = 'walking'))\n  online only\nquery walks(c: cursor ?):\n  return escorts where (.volunteerOne = viewer or .volunteerTwo = viewer) and (.status = 'assigned' or .status = 'arrived' or .status = 'walking') first 1 by .at after c\n";
+    let b = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), escorts.into())]);
+    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
+    assert!(!b.notes.iter().any(|n| n.code == "N_NEVER_COMPLETE"), "{:?}", b.notes);
+    let mut e = Engine::memory(b.schema, b.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    e.seed([
+        ("escorts".into(), object([("id", id("a")), ("student", id("s")), ("volunteerOne", id("alice")), ("volunteerTwo", Value::Null), ("status", Value::String("assigned".into())), ("at", Value::Int(1))])),
+        ("escorts".into(), object([("id", id("b")), ("student", id("s")), ("volunteerOne", id("alice")), ("volunteerTwo", id("alice")), ("status", Value::String("walking".into())), ("at", Value::Int(2))])),
+        ("escorts".into(), object([("id", id("hidden")), ("student", id("s")), ("volunteerOne", id("alice")), ("volunteerTwo", Value::Null), ("status", Value::String("completed".into())), ("at", Value::Int(3))])),
+    ]).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(walk(&mut e, "walks", "alice"), ["a", "b"]);
+
+    let grants = "table chapterAccess:\n  chapter: int\n  principal: principal\n  unique chapter, principal\n  read <- .principal = viewer\n  online only\ntable versionAccess:\n  version: id\n  principal: principal\n  unique version, principal\n  read <- .principal = viewer\n  online only\ntable versions:\n  chapter: int\n  at: int\n  read <- exists chapterAccess[.chapter, viewer] and exists versionAccess[.id, viewer]\n  online only\nquery versionsOf(chapter: int, c: cursor ?):\n  return versions where .chapter = chapter first 1 by .at after c\n";
+    let b = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), grants.into())]);
+    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
+    assert!(!b.notes.iter().any(|n| matches!(n.code.as_str(), "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE")), "{:?}", b.notes);
+    assert!(b.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"));
+    let mut e = Engine::memory(b.schema, b.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    e.seed([
+        ("chapterAccess".into(), object([("id", id("ca")), ("chapter", Value::Int(1)), ("principal", id("alice"))])),
+        ("versionAccess".into(), object([("id", id("va")), ("version", id("a")), ("principal", id("alice"))])),
+        ("versions".into(), object([("id", id("a")), ("chapter", Value::Int(1)), ("at", Value::Int(1))])),
+        ("versions".into(), object([("id", id("b")), ("chapter", Value::Int(1)), ("at", Value::Int(2))])),
+    ]).unwrap();
+    let args = |chapter| BTreeMap::from([("chapter".into(), Value::Int(chapter)), ("c".into(), Value::Null)]);
+    let r = e.query("versionsOf", Context::new("alice", args(1))).unwrap();
+    assert!(r.complete, "{r:?}");
+    assert_eq!(r.data.as_array().unwrap().iter().map(|row| row.field("id").unwrap().as_str().unwrap()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ["a"]);
+    let r = e.query("versionsOf", Context::new("bob", args(1))).unwrap();
+    assert!(r.complete && r.data.as_array().unwrap().is_empty(), "{r:?}");
+
+    // A rule probe sees its target with system authority, while the residual
+    // query probe uses ordinary read authority.  Keep PAGE out unless those
+    // observations are equivalent for a timeless readable exact point.
+    for unsafe_grant in [
+        grants.replacen("  read <- .principal = viewer", "  read <- deny", 1),
+        grants.replacen("  read <- .principal = viewer", "  until: time ?\n  expire at .until\n  read <- .principal = viewer", 1),
+        grants.replacen("  principal: principal\n  unique chapter, principal", "  principal: principal\n  active: bool\n  unique chapter, principal", 1)
+            .replace("exists chapterAccess[.chapter, viewer]", "exists chapterAccess[chapter = .chapter, principal = viewer, active = true]"),
+    ] {
+        let declined = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), unsafe_grant)]);
+        assert!(declined.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", declined.diagnostics);
+        assert!(declined.notes.iter().any(|n| n.code == "N_NEVER_COMPLETE"), "{:?}", declined.notes);
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn equality_filter_note_prints_one_matching_index_and_query() {
+    let source = "table skippers:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  read <- .principal = viewer\n  online only\ntable watches:\n  crew: principal\n  starts: time\n  by byStart: starts, id\n  read <- .crew = viewer or exists skippers[viewer]\n  online only\nquery myWatches():\n  return watches where .crew = viewer first 240 by byStart\n";
+    let b = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
+    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
+    let note = b.notes.iter().find(|note| note.code == "N_NEVER_COMPLETE").unwrap();
+    assert!(note.rewrite.contains("by byCrewStarts: crew, starts, id"), "{note}");
+    assert!(note.rewrite.contains("return watches where .crew = viewer first 240 by byCrewStarts"), "{note}");
+
+    let repaired = source.replace("  by byStart: starts, id", "  by byStart: starts, id\n  by byCrewStarts: crew, starts, id")
+        .replace("first 240 by byStart", "first 240 by byCrewStarts");
+    let b = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), repaired)]);
+    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
+    assert!(!b.notes.iter().any(|note| note.code == "N_NEVER_COMPLETE"), "{:?}", b.notes);
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn exact_vacancy_pages_publish_only_rule_visible_exhaustion() {
+    let source = "table artists:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  read <- .principal = viewer\n  online only\ntable designs:\n  oneOfKind: bool\n  at: int\n  read <- exists artists[viewer] or not .oneOfKind or not exists holds[design = .id] or exists holds[design = .id, owner = viewer]\n  online only\ntable holds:\n  design: designs\n  owner: principal\n  expiresAt: time ?\n  unique design\n  read <- .owner = viewer or exists artists[viewer]\n  expire at .expiresAt\n  online only\nquery available(c: cursor ?):\n  return designs first 1 by .at after c\n";
+    let b = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
+    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", b.diagnostics);
+    assert!(!b.notes.iter().any(|note| note.code == "N_NEVER_COMPLETE"), "{:?}", b.notes);
+    assert!(b.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"));
+    let mut e = Engine::memory(b.schema, b.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    e.seed([
+        ("artists".into(), object([("id", id("artist")), ("principal", id("art"))])),
+        ("designs".into(), object([("id", id("a")), ("oneOfKind", Value::Bool(false)), ("at", Value::Int(1))])),
+        ("designs".into(), object([("id", id("b")), ("oneOfKind", Value::Bool(true)), ("at", Value::Int(2))])),
+        ("designs".into(), object([("id", id("c")), ("oneOfKind", Value::Bool(true)), ("at", Value::Int(3))])),
+        ("holds".into(), object([("id", id("h")), ("design", id("c")), ("owner", id("alice")), ("expiresAt", Value::Null)])),
+    ]).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(walk(&mut e, "available", "bob"), ["a", "b"]);
+    assert_eq!(walk(&mut e, "available", "alice"), ["a", "b", "c"]);
+    assert_eq!(walk(&mut e, "available", "art"), ["a", "b", "c"]);
+
+    let non_point = source.replace("  design: designs\n", "  design: designs ?\n")
+        .replace(" or exists holds[design = .id, owner = viewer]", "");
+    let declined = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), non_point)]);
+    assert!(declined.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", declined.diagnostics);
+    assert!(declined.notes.iter().any(|note| note.code == "N_NEVER_COMPLETE"));
+}

+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — older devices refuse PAGE5's validator
+// interpretation instead of executing fixed residual or vacancy lanes as v3.
+#[test]
+fn page5_plans_require_branch_pages_v4_from_the_device() {
+    let vacancy = "table holds:
+  item: designs
+  owner: principal
+  unique item
+  read <- .owner = viewer
+  online only
+table designs:
+  owner: principal
+  read <- not exists holds[item = .id] or .owner = viewer
+  online only
+query available(c: cursor ?):
+  return designs first 1 by .id after c
+";
+    let fixed = "table roles:
+  bucket: int
+  principal: principal
+  unique bucket, principal
+  read <- .principal = viewer
+  online only
+table access:
+  item: items
+  principal: principal
+  unique item, principal
+  read <- .principal = viewer
+  online only
+table items:
+  bucket: int
+  at: int
+  read <- exists roles[.bucket, viewer] and exists access[.id, viewer]
+  online only
+query page(bucket: int, c: cursor ?):
+  return items where .bucket = bucket first 1 by .at after c
+";
+    let product = "table items:
+  owner: principal
+  helper: principal
+  status: text <=10
+  at: int
+  read <- (.owner = viewer or .helper = viewer) and (.status = 'open' or .status = 'active')
+  online only
+query page(c: cursor ?):
+  return items first 1 by .at after c
+";
+    for (name, source) in [("fixed", fixed), ("vacancy", vacancy)] {
+        let (_, backend) = compile(source);
+        assert!(backend.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"), "{name}");
+        let required = snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&backend.schema.public_projection());
+        let mut old = snapback4_core::device_protocol::DeviceEngine::current();
+        old.version = "0.4.28-page4".into();
+        old.capabilities.retain(|capability| capability != "branch-pages-v4");
+        let refusal = snapback4_core::device_protocol::check(Some(&old), &required).unwrap_err();
+        assert_eq!(refusal.code, "E_DEVICE_VERSION", "{name}");
+        assert!(refusal.message.contains("branch-pages-v4"), "{name}: {refusal:?}");
+    }
+    let (_, product_backend) = compile(product);
+    assert!(!product_backend.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"),
+        "bounded products reuse the existing branch validator");
+}
+

+
+/// PAGE5's three new planner families are generated independently so their
+/// evidence does not depend on the broader widening stream selecting a page.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — bounded products, fixed+varying grants and exact vacancy all receive generated cases.
+pub fn page5(seed: u64, shape: u64) -> Case {
+    let mut case = base(seed);
+    let mut rng = Rng::new(seed ^ 0x5041_4745_355f_454e ^ shape);
+    case.program.body = Body::Read { kind: Read::Page, take: 1 + rng.pick(3) as u32,
+        descending: rng.pick(2) == 0, filter: false, owner: false, project: None };
+    case.program.prelude.clear(); case.steps.clear(); case.program.lets.clear();
+    case.program.order = Some(vec!["id".into()]); case.program.named_page_order = None;
+    case.program.principal_arg = None; case.program.residual = None; case.program.guard = None;
+    case.program.search_term = None; case.program.membership_named_reordered = false;
+    case.program.boolean_grant_normalized = false; case.program.kids = None;
+    case.program.tables[0].effects = None; case.program.tables[0].residency = Residency::Online;
+    let bucket = case.args["bucket"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0);
+    match shape % 3 {
+        0 => {
+            case.program.tables[0].rule = Rule::ProductScope;
+            case.program.domain = Some("(.owner = viewer or .helper = viewer) and (.status = 'open' or .status = 'active')".into());
+            let items = &mut case.program.tables[0];
+            if !items.columns.iter().any(|column| column.name == "helper") { items.columns.push(column("helper", Type::Principal)); }
+            if !items.columns.iter().any(|column| column.name == "status") { items.columns.push(column("status", Type::Text)); }
+            for (table, row) in &mut case.rows { if table == "items" {
+                row["helper"] = json!(if rng.pick(2) == 0 { "alice" } else { "bob" });
+                row["status"] = json!(["open", "active", "closed"][rng.pick(3) as usize]);
+            }}
+        }
+        1 => {
+            case.program.tables[0].rule = Rule::FixedPerRow;
+            case.program.domain = Some(".bucket = bucket".into());
+            case.program.order = Some(vec!["score".into()]);
+            id_membership(&mut case);
+            let access = case.program.tables.iter_mut().find(|table| table.name == "access").unwrap();
+            access.rule = Rule::Member; access.effects = None; access.residency = Residency::Online;
+            access.expire = None; access.not_before = None; access.caps.clear();
+            let roles = Table { name: "roles".into(), columns: vec![column("bucket", Type::Int), column("principal", Type::Principal)],
+                keys: vec![("byBucketPrincipal".into(), vec!["bucket".into(), "principal".into()], true)], caps: vec![],
+                rule: Rule::Member, residency: Residency::Online, effects: None, expire: None, not_before: None };
+            case.program.tables.push(roles);
+            if rng.pick(4) != 0 { case.rows.push(("roles".into(), json!({"id":format!("role-{seed}"), "bucket":bucket, "principal":case.principal}))); }
+        }
+        _ => {
+            case.program.tables[0].rule = Rule::Vacancy;
+            case.program.domain = Some("true".into());
+            id_membership(&mut case);
+            let access = case.program.tables.iter_mut().find(|table| table.name == "access").unwrap();
+            access.rule = Rule::Member; access.effects = None; access.residency = Residency::Online;
+            access.expire = None; access.not_before = None; access.caps.clear();
+            access.keys.retain(|(name, _, _)| name == "byItem");
+            access.keys.iter_mut().for_each(|(_, columns, unique)| { *columns = vec!["item".into()]; *unique = true; });
+            let mut items = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
+            case.rows.retain(|(table, row)| table != "access" || items.insert(row["item"].to_string()));
+        }
+    }
+    case
+}
+
+pub(crate) fn page5_features(case: &Case) -> Vec<String> {
+    let family = match case.program.tables[0].rule {
+        Rule::ProductScope => Some("product-scope"),
+        Rule::FixedPerRow => Some("fixed-per-row"),
+        Rule::Vacancy => Some("vacancy"),
+        _ => None,
+    };
+    family.map(|name| vec![format!("page5/{name}")]).unwrap_or_default()
+}

+
+#[test]
+fn page5_generated_families_compile_and_compare_natively() {
+    let mut seen = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
+    for shape in 0..3 {
+        for seed in 0..8 {
+            let case = snapback4_difftest::generate::page5(seed, shape);
+            seen.extend(snapback4_difftest::coverage::features(&case).into_iter().filter(|f| f.starts_with("page5/")));
+            let source = case.program.source();
+            execute::compile(&source).unwrap_or_else(|error| panic!("shape {shape} seed {seed}: {error}\n{source}"));
+            let (verdict, executions) = oracle::repeat(&case, &source).unwrap();
+            assert!(matches!(verdict, Verdict::Pass) && executions >= 2,
+                "shape {shape} seed {seed}: {verdict:?}\n{source}");
+        }
+    }
+    assert_eq!(seen, ["page5/fixed-per-row", "page5/product-scope", "page5/vacancy"].map(String::from).into());
+}

+
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — simulator execution covers the PAGE5 fixed
+// point guard beside a per-row membership and exact vacancy exhaustion.
+#[test]
+fn page5_product_fixed_membership_and_vacancy_publish_complete_pages() {
+    let product = bound("table items:
+  owner: principal
+  helper: principal
+  status: text <=10
+  at: int
+  read <- (.owner = viewer or .helper = viewer) and (.status = 'open' or .status = 'active')
+  online only
+query page():
+  return items first 10 by .at
+");
+    assert!(!product.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"));
+    let mut engine = Engine::memory(product.schema, product.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    engine.seed([
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("a")), ("owner", id("alice")), ("helper", id("bob")), ("status", Value::String("open".into())), ("at", Value::Int(1))])),
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("b")), ("owner", id("bob")), ("helper", id("alice")), ("status", Value::String("active".into())), ("at", Value::Int(2))])),
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("c")), ("owner", id("alice")), ("helper", id("bob")), ("status", Value::String("closed".into())), ("at", Value::Int(3))])),
+    ]).unwrap();
+    let result = engine.query("page", context("alice", &[])).unwrap();
+    assert!(result.complete && result.next.is_none(), "{result:?}");
+    let ids: Vec<_> = result.data.as_array().unwrap().iter().map(|row| row.field("id").unwrap().clone()).collect();
+    assert_eq!(ids, [id("a"), id("b")]);
+
+    let fixed = bound("table chapters:
+  bucket: int
+  principal: principal
+  unique bucket, principal
+  read <- .principal = viewer
+  online only
+table access:
+  item: items
+  principal: principal
+  unique item, principal
+  read <- .principal = viewer
+  online only
+table items:
+  bucket: int
+  at: int
+  read <- exists chapters[.bucket, viewer] and exists access[.id, viewer]
+  online only
+query page(bucket: int, c: cursor ?):
+  return items where .bucket = bucket first 1 by .at after c
+");
+    assert!(fixed.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"));
+    let mut engine = Engine::memory(fixed.schema, fixed.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    engine.seed([
+        ("chapters".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("chapter")), ("bucket", Value::Int(1)), ("principal", id("alice"))])),
+        ("access".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("access")), ("item", id("visible")), ("principal", id("alice"))])),
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("visible")), ("bucket", Value::Int(1)), ("at", Value::Int(1))])),
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("hidden")), ("bucket", Value::Int(1)), ("at", Value::Int(2))])),
+    ]).unwrap();
+    let result = engine.query("page", context("alice", &[("bucket", Value::Int(1)), ("c", Value::Null)])).unwrap();
+    assert!(result.complete && result.next.is_none(), "{result:?}");
+    assert_eq!(result.data.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &id("visible"));
+
+    let vacancy = bound("table holds:
+  item: designs
+  owner: principal
+  unique item
+  read <- .owner = viewer
+  online only
+table designs:
+  owner: principal
+  read <- not exists holds[item = .id] or .owner = viewer
+  online only
+query available(c: cursor ?):
+  return designs first 1 by .id after c
+");
+    assert!(vacancy.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"));
+    let mut engine = Engine::memory(vacancy.schema, vacancy.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    engine.seed([
+        ("holds".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("hold")), ("item", id("held")), ("owner", id("alice"))])),
+        ("designs".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("free")), ("owner", id("alice"))])),
+        ("designs".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("held")), ("owner", id("alice"))])),
+    ]).unwrap();
+    let result = engine.query("available", context("bob", &[("c", Value::Null)])).unwrap();
+    assert!(result.complete && result.next.is_none(), "{result:?}");
+    assert_eq!(result.data.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &id("free"));
+}
 // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — only live memberships may synthesize PAGE target lanes.

diff --git a/snapback4/lean/Quarry/Eval.lean b/snapback4/lean/Quarry/Eval.lean

@@ -568,6 +568,35 @@ def laneFits (order : List String) (eqs pins : List (String × Expr)) : Bool :=
 def laneDecided (td : TableDecl) (order : List String) (eqs pins : List (String × Expr)) : Bool :=
   laneFits order eqs pins && decidedPins td ((eqs ++ pins).filter fun p => p.1 != "id" && !order.contains p.1)
 
+/-- PAGE5's fixed half of a fixed-plus-varying grant: after substituting the
+page pins, this is one timeless, required exact point whose ordinary read
+rule discloses the same answer to the caller. -/
+def fixedPointGuard (sch : Schema) (outer : TableDecl)
+    (pins : List (String × Expr)) : Rule → Bool
+  | r@(.probe t point key) => match sch.table? t with
+    | some source =>
+      let columns := key.map (·.1)
+      key.length == point.length
+        && point.eraseDups.length == point.length
+        && point.all columns.contains
+        && (point == ["id"] || source.uniques.contains point)
+        && point.all (fun name => name == "id" || source.cols.any (fun col => col.name == name && !col.nullable))
+        && source.notBefore.isNone && source.expire.isNone
+        && r.usableGuard sch outer pins
+    | none => false
+  | _ => false
+
+/-- PAGE5's varying half has exactly one outer-row key component and otherwise
+uses PAGE's existing readable, total membership witness. -/
+def varyingPointGrant (sch : Schema) (pins : List (String × Expr))
+    (order : List String) : Rule → Bool
+  | .probe t point key => membershipEligible sch t point key
+      && (membershipColumns key).any fun pair =>
+        !pins.any (·.1 == pair.2)
+          && (!order.contains pair.2 || pair.2 == "id" && order == ["id"])
+          && laneFits order [(pair.2, .viewer)] pins
+  | _ => false
+
 /-- Each positive alternative either has a prefix-decided rule, its own
 readable guard, or one exact membership witness with every remaining
 conjunct true by its pins. -/
@@ -593,6 +622,9 @@ def compoundDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl) (pins : List (String × Expr

                   && laneFits order ((pair.2, .viewer) :: extra) pins
               | _ => false
+            | [a, b] =>
+                (fixedPointGuard sch td ps a && varyingPointGrant sch ps order b)
+                  || (fixedPointGuard sch td ps b && varyingPointGrant sch ps order a)
             | _ => false)
 
 
@@ -621,6 +653,17 @@ def scopedWitnessDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl)
               && laneFits order [(pair.2, .viewer)] pins
     | _ => false
 
+/-- PAGE5's explicit vacancy: a top-level negated probe of one required
+exact point by the focal row's intrinsic id. The native lane still checks the
+whole read rule per row; this predicate governs only its exhaustion verdict. -/
+def vacancyDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl) : Bool :=
+  td.read.disjuncts.any fun r => match r with
+    | .not (.probe t [point] [(key, .col .self "id")]) =>
+        point == key && (sch.table? t).any fun source =>
+          source.uniques.contains [point]
+            && (source.cols.find? (·.name == point)).all (!·.nullable)
+    | _ => false
+
 /-- The pins the single-index planner keeps: the page's equalities and bare
 boolean conjuncts, except `id` and an order column other than the first,
 which stay seeks (`Requests::read`; GPT round 3, finding 3). -/
@@ -705,7 +748,8 @@ def branchDecided (sch : Schema) (t : String) (pins : List (String × Expr))
       | none => false
     let case4 := guards && !reservedMembership && !pinnedMembership && alts.isNone && compoundDecided sch td pins order
     let witnessScope := guards && pins.all (fun p => p.2.total) && scopedWitnessDecided sch td pins fs order
-    (total && (case1 || case2)) || (readFree && (case3 || case4)) || witnessScope
+    let vacancy := guards && pins.isEmpty && fs.isEmpty && vacancyDecided sch td
+    (total && (case1 || case2)) || (readFree && (case3 || case4)) || witnessScope || vacancy
 
 def keyValue (k : Key) : Value := .arr (k.map .s)

+def page5Product : Schema := { tables := [
+  { name := "items", cols := [c "owner", c "helper", c "status", c "at"]
+    read := .and
+      (.or (.eq (self "owner") .viewer) (.eq (self "helper") .viewer))
+      (.or (.eq (self "status") (.lit (T "open"))) (.eq (self "status") (.lit (T "active")))) }] }
+
+def page5FixedPerRow : Schema := { tables := [
+  { name := "roles", cols := [c "bucket", c "principal"], uniques := [["bucket", "principal"]]
+    read := .eq (self "principal") .viewer },
+  { name := "access", cols := [c "item", c "principal"], uniques := [["item", "principal"]]
+    read := .eq (self "principal") .viewer },
+  { name := "items", cols := [c "bucket", c "at"]
+    read := .and
+      (.probe "roles" ["bucket", "principal"] [("bucket", self "bucket"), ("principal", .viewer)])
+      (.probe "access" ["item", "principal"] [("item", self "id"), ("principal", .viewer)]) }] }
+
+def page5Vacancy : Schema := { tables := [
+  { name := "holds", cols := [c "design", c "owner"], uniques := [["design"]], read := .eq (self "owner") .viewer },
+  { name := "designs", cols := [c "owner", c "at"],
+    read := .or (.not (.probe "holds" ["design"] [("design", self "id")])) (.eq (self "owner") .viewer) }] }
+def page5VacancyExamples : List Example := [
+  { name := "page5-bounded-product"
+    source := "LLP 3000.036.001 §18: two principal pins times two status pins retain both predicates in four complete lanes"
+    case := {
+      schema := page5Product
+      store := mkStore [("items", [
+        mkRow "a" [("owner", I "alice"), ("helper", I "bob"), ("status", T "open"), ("at", N 1)],
+        mkRow "b" [("owner", I "bob"), ("helper", I "alice"), ("status", T "active"), ("at", N 2)],
+        mkRow "c" [("owner", I "alice"), ("helper", I "bob"), ("status", T "closed"), ("at", N 3)],
+        mkRow "d" [("owner", I "bob"), ("helper", I "bob"), ("status", T "open"), ("at", N 4)]])]
+      viewer := alice
+      program := query [.ret (.page "items" [] none ["at"] false 10 none)] }
+    tables := []
+    expected := r#"{"kind":"answer","data":[{"id":"a","owner":"alice","helper":"bob","status":"open","at":1},{"id":"b","owner":"bob","helper":"alice","status":"active","at":2}],"complete":true,"next":null}"# },
+  { name := "page5-fixed-plus-per-row"
+    source := "LLP 3000.036.001 §18: one readable fixed point may guard one varying per-row membership lane"
+    case := {

+      store := mkStore [
+        ("roles", [mkRow "r" [("bucket", N 1), ("principal", I "alice")]]),
+        ("access", [mkRow "x" [("item", I "a"), ("principal", I "alice")],

+        ("items", [mkRow "a" [("bucket", N 1), ("at", N 1)],
+          mkRow "b" [("bucket", N 1), ("at", N 2)],
+          mkRow "c" [("bucket", N 2), ("at", N 3)]])]
+      viewer := alice
+      program := query [.ret (.page "items" [("bucket", int 1)] none ["at"] false 10 none)] }
+    tables := []
+    expected := r#"{"kind":"answer","data":[{"id":"a","bucket":1,"at":1}],"complete":true,"next":null}"# },
+  { name := "page5-exact-vacancy"
+    source := "LLP 3000.036.001 §18: explicit exact-point vacancy makes private server exhaustion observable"
+    case := {
+      schema := page5Vacancy
+      store := mkStore [("holds", [mkRow "h" [("design", I "held"), ("owner", I "alice")]]),
+        ("designs", [mkRow "free" [("owner", I "alice"), ("at", N 1)],
+          mkRow "held" [("owner", I "alice"), ("at", N 2)]])]
+      viewer := bob

+    tables := []

+]

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	//! Bounded positive DNF of a read rule. Each conjunct is retained in its
     2	//! range; a guard opens only that range, never the other rule branches.
     3	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#16 — complete compound pages use the same bounded merge and discovery fence.
     4	use super::{catalog::TableInfo, intent::{self, Requests}, intent_branches as branches};
     5	use crate::ast::*;
     6	use snapback4_core::ir::{BRANCH_LANE, GUARDED_LANE, SYSTEM_LANE, VACANCY_LANE};
     7	use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
     8	
     9	type Tables = BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>;
    10	
    11	/// Comparisons and readable static points have a total logical predicate.
    12	/// A physical point can still cut: the merge frontier excludes an unfinished
    13	/// filter candidate, so continuation retries it rather than skipping it.
    14	pub(super) fn total_readable(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &BTreeSet<(String, String)>, focal: &TableInfo, value: &Expr) -> bool {
    15	    if branches::total(value) { return true; }
    16	    match value {
    17	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" || op == "or" =>
    18	            total_readable(tables, synthesized, focal, left) && total_readable(tables, synthesized, focal, right),
    19	        Expr::Exists { read: probe, .. } if branches::columns_within(value, &[]) => {
    20	            if probe.range.is_some() || probe.filter.is_some() || probe.matching.is_some() || !probe.seeks.is_empty() { return false; }
    21	            if !probe.keys.iter().all(|key| fixed_key_total(&key.value)) { return false; }
    22	            let Some(target) = tables.get(&probe.table) else { return false };
    23	            if target.through.is_some() || target.decl.not_before.is_some() || target.decl.expire.is_some() { return false; }
    24	            let Some(columns) = super::intent_lanes::probe_columns(tables, synthesized, focal, probe) else { return false };
    25	            if !target.indexes.iter().any(|index| target.total_unique(index) && index.columns.iter().all(|column| columns.contains(column))) { return false; }
    26	            let mut point = probe.clone();
    27	            for (key, column) in point.keys.iter_mut().zip(columns) { key.column = Some(column); }
    28	            target.decl.public.is_some() || branches::read_rule(target).is_some_and(|source_rule|
    29	                branches::by_form(source_rule, &point) || crate::print::expression(source_rule) == crate::print::expression(value))
    30	        }
    31	        _ => false,
    32	    }
    33	}
    34	
    35	/// Distribution must not evaluate a fallible key on an overlapping party's
    36	/// row where the original short circuit skipped it. Signed numeric literals
    37	/// are bound constants; arithmetic, assertions, calls and reads stay put.
    38	fn fixed_key_total(value: &Expr) -> bool {
    39	    matches!(value, Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. })
    40	        || matches!(value, Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op == "-" && matches!(value.as_ref(), Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Number(_), .. }))
    41	}
    42	
    43	pub(super) fn expand(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {
    44	    let range = read.range.as_ref()?;
    45	    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
    46	    if read.index.is_some() || !read.keys.is_empty() || read.matching.is_some() || !read.computed.is_empty() || read.cursor_prefix.is_some()
    47	        || table.maintained.is_some() || table.through.is_some() || table.decl.ephemeral.is_some() || !table.captures.is_empty() || table.decl.public.is_some()
    48	        || read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| !branches::total(filter)) { return None; }
    49	    let rule = branches::read_rule(table)?;
    50	    if let Some(merge) = vacancy(tables, synthesized, requests, read) { return Some(merge); }
    51	    // Keep the published simple plans and descriptors. This pass handles
    52	    // compound rules, not the existing party/grant or exact membership rule.
    53	    if !compound(rule) && !matches!(rule, Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "or") { return None; }
    54	    let leaves = dnf(table, rule)?;
    55	    if leaves.len() + 1 > branches::MAX_BRANCHES { return None; }
    56	    let mut candidate = requests.clone();
    57	    let errors = candidate.error_count();
    58	    let mut lanes = Vec::new();
    59	    let mut order = None;
    60	    let mut system = read.clone(); candidate.read(tables, &mut system, false);
    61	    if branches::decided(tables, &system) { return None; }
    62	    branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, system, SYSTEM_LANE, None, None, read.filter.clone())?;
    63	    for leaf in leaves {
    64	        let mut pins = Vec::new();
    65	        let mut probes = Vec::new();
    66	        for atom in &leaf {
    67	            if let Expr::Exists { .. } = atom {
    68	                // Static points are fixed guards too; omitting them from the
    69	                // retained residual would weaken the authored conjunction.
    70	                probes.push(atom.clone());
    71	            } else if intent::equality(atom, table).is_some() {
    72	                pins.push(atom.clone());
    73	            } else if matches!(atom, Expr::Unary { .. }) && !branches::columns_within(atom, &[]) {
    74	                // A bare negated boolean pin was handled above. Negated
    75	                // probes are outside this positive distribution classifier.
    76	                return None;
    77	            } else if !branches::columns_within(atom, &[]) { return None; }
    78	        }
    79	        let mut lane_read = read.clone();
    80	        if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { pins.push(*filter.clone()); }
    81	        lane_read.filter = intent::conjunction(pins);
    82	        // A conjunction may require one grant fixed by the authored page
    83	        // prefix and one per-row grant discovered by PAGE.  Retain the fixed
    84	        // point as an exact residual predicate on every discovered target
    85	        // lane; the bound validator independently verifies that predicate
    86	        // against the original rule before admitting the merge.
    87	        // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — a pinned grant may guard one varying per-row grant.
    88	        let mut fixed_read = lane_read.clone();
    89	        let mut fixed_candidate = candidate.clone();
    90	        fixed_candidate.plan_lane(tables, &mut fixed_read);
    91	        let pinned: BTreeMap<_, _> = fixed_read.keys.iter().filter_map(|key|
    92	            key.column.as_ref().filter(|_| fixed_key_total(&key.value)).map(|column| (column.clone(), key.value.clone()))).collect();
    93	        let mut witness = None;
    94	        let mut fixed_guards = Vec::new();
    95	        for mut probe in probes {
    96	            let fields: Vec<_> = match &probe { Expr::Exists { read, .. } => read.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| intent::column(&key.value)).collect(), _ => Vec::new() };
    97	            // A point with no outer fields is already fixed; a point with
    98	            // outer fields becomes fixed only after every field is pinned.
    99	            if fields.iter().all(|field| pinned.contains_key(*field)) {
   100	                super::intent_walk::expression(&mut probe, &mut |value| {
   101	                    if let Some(replacement) = intent::column(value).and_then(|column| pinned.get(column)) { *value = replacement.clone(); }
   102	                });
   103	                // The residual runs as an ordinary authored read, while the
   104	                // rule probe itself has system visibility.  They are
   105	                // equivalent only for an exact, timeless point the caller is
   106	                // structurally allowed to read.
   107	                if !total_readable(tables, synthesized, table, &probe) { return None; }

   115	            let mut fixed_read = lane_read.clone();
   116	            let mut fixed_candidate = candidate.clone();
   117	            fixed_candidate.plan_lane(tables, &mut fixed_read);
   118	            let pinned: BTreeMap<_, _> = fixed_read.keys.iter().filter_map(|key|
   119	                key.column.as_ref().filter(|_| fixed_key_total(&key.value)).map(|column| (column.clone(), key.value.clone()))).collect();
   120	            let target_pinned = if let Expr::Exists { read: probe, .. } = &witness {
   121	                let fields: Vec<_> = probe.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| intent::column(&key.value)).collect();
   122	                !fields.is_empty() && fields.iter().all(|field| pinned.contains_key(*field))
   123	            } else { false };
   124	            if target_pinned {
   125	                candidate.plan_lane(tables, &mut lane_read);
   126	                branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, lane_read, GUARDED_LANE, None, None, read.filter.clone())?;
   127	                continue;
   128	            }
   129	            // Reuse PAGE's complete source admission on exactly this witness.
   130	            // The real schema still has the entire compound rule; the bound
   131	            // validator verifies its remaining conjuncts from these pins.
   132	            if leaf.iter().any(|atom| !matches!(atom, Expr::Exists { .. }) && intent::equality(atom, table).is_none()) { return None; }
   133	            super::intent_walk::expression(&mut witness, &mut |value| {
   134	                if let Some(replacement) = intent::column(value).and_then(|column| pinned.get(column)) { *value = replacement.clone(); }
   135	            });
   136	            let mut local = tables.clone();
   137	            local.get_mut(&read.table)?.decl.rules.iter_mut().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read)?.value = witness;
   138	            let merge = branches::membership(&local, synthesized, &mut candidate, &lane_read)?;
   139	            for mut lane in merge.lanes.into_iter().filter(|lane| lane.branch.as_deref() != Some(SYSTEM_LANE)) {
   140	                if !fixed_guards.is_empty() {
   141	                    let planned = lane.planned.as_mut()?;
   142	                    let mut predicates = planned.filter.take().map(|filter| vec![*filter]).unwrap_or_default();
   143	                    predicates.extend(fixed_guards.iter().cloned());
   144	                    planned.filter = intent::conjunction(predicates);
   145	                }
   146	                if order.as_ref() != Some(&lane.planned.as_ref()?.order_keys) { return None; }
   147	                lanes.push(lane);
   148	                if lanes.len() > branches::MAX_BRANCHES { return None; }
   149	            }
   150	        } else {
   151	            candidate.plan_lane(tables, &mut lane_read);
   152	            let branch = if branches::decided(tables, &lane_read) { BRANCH_LANE } else { GUARDED_LANE };
   153	            // Binding repeats guard readability against the real core schema.
   154	            // Do not put a grant probe in the residual row predicate.
   155	            branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, lane_read, branch, None, None, read.filter.clone())?;
   156	        }
   157	    }
   158	    if candidate.error_count() != errors { return None; }
   159	    *requests = candidate;
   160	    Some(MergeExpr { view_order: Vec::new(), table: read.table.clone(), matching: None, filter: None, index: String::new(), order: range.order,
   161	        take: range.take, cursor: range.cursor.clone(), lanes, site: read.site.clone() })
   162	}
   163	
   164	/// A top-level `not exists point[.id]` explicitly makes vacancy observable.
   165	/// One server lane checks the original rule for every candidate and may report
   166	/// exhaustion only after the physical range ends. It carries no ordinary
   167	/// filter because rule probes use wider system visibility.
   168	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — exact vacancy is the sole non-uniform exhaustion lane.
   169	pub(super) fn vacancy(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests,
   170	    read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {
   171	    let range = read.range.as_ref()?;
   172	    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
   173	    let rule = branches::read_rule(table)?;
   174	    if read.filter.is_some() || read.index.is_some() || !read.keys.is_empty() || read.matching.is_some()
   175	        || !read.computed.is_empty() || read.cursor_prefix.is_some()
   176	        || (table.decl.sync.is_some() && !table.decl.online_only) { return None; }
   177	    fn alternatives<'a>(value: &'a Expr, out: &mut Vec<&'a Expr>) {
   178	        match value {
   179	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => { alternatives(left, out); alternatives(right, out); }
   180	            other => out.push(other),
   181	        }
   182	    }
   183	    let mut disjuncts = Vec::new(); alternatives(rule, &mut disjuncts);
   184	    let admitted = disjuncts.iter().any(|value| {
   185	        let Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } = value else { return false };
   186	        let Expr::Exists { read: probe, .. } = value.as_ref() else { return false };
   187	        if op != "not" || probe.range.is_some() || probe.filter.is_some() || probe.matching.is_some()
   188	            || !probe.seeks.is_empty() || probe.keys.len() != 1 || intent::column(&probe.keys[0].value) != Some("id") { return false; }
   189	        let Some(target) = tables.get(&probe.table) else { return false };
   190	        let Some(columns) = super::intent_lanes::probe_columns(tables, synthesized, table, probe) else { return false };
   191	        target.indexes.iter().any(|index| target.total_unique(index) && index.columns == columns)
   192	    });
   193	    if !admitted { return None; }
   194	    let mut candidate = requests.clone(); let errors = candidate.error_count();
   195	    let mut system = read.clone(); candidate.read(tables, &mut system, false);
   196	    let mut lane = read.clone(); candidate.plan_lane(tables, &mut lane);
   197	    let mut lanes = Vec::with_capacity(2); let mut order = None;
   198	    branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, system, SYSTEM_LANE, None, None, None)?;
   199	    branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, lane, VACANCY_LANE, None, None, None)?;
   200	    if candidate.error_count() != errors { return None; }
   201	    *requests = candidate;
   202	    Some(MergeExpr { view_order: Vec::new(), table: read.table.clone(), matching: None, filter: None,
   203	        index: String::new(), order: range.order, take: range.take, cursor: range.cursor.clone(), lanes, site: read.site.clone() })
   204	}
   205	
   206	fn compound(rule: &Expr) -> bool {
   207	    match rule {
   208	        Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "and" || op == "!=" => true,
   209	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => compound(left) || compound(right),
   210	        _ => false,
   211	    }
   212	}
   213	
   214	/// At most seven positive alternatives plus the system range. A nullable
   215	/// enum's `!= null` is exactly its finite set of non-null values.
   216	fn dnf(table: &TableInfo, value: &Expr) -> Option<Vec<Vec<Expr>>> {
   217	    let out = match value {
   218	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => {
   219	            let mut out = dnf(table, left)?; out.extend(dnf(table, right)?); out
   220	        }
   221	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => {
   222	            let a = dnf(table, left)?; let b = dnf(table, right)?;
   223	            if a.len().saturating_mul(b.len()) >= branches::MAX_BRANCHES { return None; }
   224	            a.into_iter().flat_map(|a| b.iter().map(move |b| { let mut out = a.clone(); out.extend(b.clone()); out })).collect()
   225	        }
   226	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, site } if op == "!=" => {
   227	            let (column, term) = if let Some(column) = intent::column(left) { (column, right) } else { (intent::column(right)?, left) };
   228	            let kind = table.columns.get(column)?;
   229	            let TypeKind::Enum { variants } = &kind.kind else { return None; };
   230	            let include = |variant: &String| match term.as_ref() {
   231	                Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Null, .. } if kind.optional => Some(true),
   232	                Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Str(excluded), .. } if !kind.optional => Some(variant != excluded),
   233	                _ => None,
   234	            };
   235	            if variants.iter().any(|variant| include(variant).is_none()) { return None; }
   236	            variants.iter().filter(|variant| include(variant).unwrap()).map(|variant| vec![Expr::Binary { op: "=".into(), left: Box::new(Expr::Member {
   237	                base: Box::new(Expr::Focal { site: site.clone() }), name: column.into(), site: site.clone() }),
   238	                right: Box::new(Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Str(variant.clone()), site: site.clone() }), site: site.clone() }]).collect()
   239	        }
   240	        other => vec![vec![other.clone()]],
   241	    };
   242	    (out.len() < branches::MAX_BRANCHES).then_some(out)
   243	}

     1	//! A query page whose predicate, or whose table's read rule, is a
     2	//! disjunction of branches each pinning an index prefix that decides the read
     3	//! rule plans as the merge of the branch ranges (LLP 3000.036.001): `tasks where
     4	//! .owner = viewer or .assignee = viewer last 50 by .at after c`, or the same
     5	//! list read through `read <- .owner = viewer or .assignee = viewer`. A page
     6	//! its own pins already decide is never rewritten, so no complete plan, index

     8	use super::{catalog::TableInfo, intent::{self, Requests}};
     9	use crate::ast::*;
    10	use crate::diag::Site;

    14	/// At most this many branches: each is an index range read ahead by one
    15	/// page, and each may be an index the planner synthesizes.
    16	pub(super) const MAX_BRANCHES: usize = 8;
    17	
    18	type Tables = BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>;
    19	
    20	/// One branch's conjuncts, and whether it is the grant branch.
    21	type Branch = (Vec<Expr>, bool);
    22	
    23	/// Only turn an implicit whole-group read into a page when the original
    24	/// prefix has a stored bound. A party cap does not bound a staff's all-row
    25	/// branch. The original bound applies to the union, rather than each party.
    26	fn bounded_page(tables: &Tables, requests: &Requests, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<ReadExpr> {
    27	    if read.range.is_some() { return Some(read.clone()); }
    28	    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
    29	    let mut planned = read.clone();
    30	    let mut probe = requests.clone();
    31	    probe.read(tables, &mut planned, false);
    32	    let pinned: Vec<String> = planned.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| key.column.clone()).collect();
    33	    let cap = table.decl.caps.iter().filter(|cap| cap.by.iter().all(|column| pinned.contains(column))
    34	        && !cap.by.iter().any(|column| table.decl.columns.iter().any(|field| &field.name == column
    35	            && field.on_delete.as_ref().is_some_and(|(action, _)| action == &OnDelete::SetNull))))
    36	        .map(|cap| cap.max).chain(table.indexes.iter().filter(|index| table.total_unique(index))
    37	            .filter(|index| index.columns.iter().all(|column| pinned.contains(column))).map(|_| 1)).min()?;
    38	    if u64::from(cap) > snapback4_core::Ceilings::default().examined { return None; }
    39	    let mut page = read.clone();
    40	    page.range = Some(RangeSpec { order: Order::Asc, take: cap, cursor: None, site: read.site.clone() });
    41	    Some(page)
    42	}

    44	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#2 — the rule: where-branches first, then the read rule's parties and readable grants.
    45	pub(super) fn expand(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {
    46	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#16 — implicit whole-group reads retain their proven schema bound.
    47	    let mut authored = read.clone();
    48	    // A named plain range states the same column order. Normalize only a
    49	    // speculative merge input; a declined candidate retains the authored read.
    50	    if authored.range.is_some() && authored.keys.is_empty() && authored.matching.is_none()
    51	        && authored.computed.is_empty() && authored.cursor_prefix.is_none()
    52	        && let Some(index) = authored.index.as_ref().and_then(|name| tables.get(&authored.table)?.indexes.iter().find(|index| &index.name == name))
    53	        && index.is_plain() {
    54	        authored.order_keys = index.columns.clone(); authored.index = None;
    55	    }
    56	    let normalized = bounded_page(tables, requests, &authored)?;
    57	    let read = &normalized;
    58	    // Vacancy is an exact negative lane, rather than a generic positive
    59	    // grant branch. Classify it before the positive branch planner.
    60	    // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — exact vacancy retains a full private scan.
    61	    if let Some(merge) = super::intent_compound::vacancy(tables, synthesized, requests, read) { return Some(merge); }
    62	    if let Some(merge) = scoped_membership(tables, synthesized, requests, read) { return Some(merge); }
    63	    if let Some(merge) = membership(tables, synthesized, requests, read) { return Some(merge); }
    64	    // An exact owner-or-membership rule belongs wholly to PAGE. If one of its
    65	    // stricter eligibility checks declines, keep the original scan instead of
    66	    // reinterpreting its probe as a generic grant branch.
    67	    if owner_membership_shape(tables, synthesized, read) && !pinned_memberships(tables, synthesized, read) { return None; }
    68	    let range = read.range.as_ref()?;
    69	    let Some(branches) = branches(tables, synthesized, requests, read, true) else {
    70	        return super::intent_compound::expand(tables, synthesized, requests, read);
    71	    };
    72	    let mut candidate = requests.clone();
    73	    let errors = candidate.error_count();
    74	    let mut lanes = Vec::with_capacity(branches.len());
    75	    let mut order: Option<Vec<String>> = None;
    76	    for (conjuncts, grant) in branches {
    77	        let mut lane = read.clone();
    78	        lane.filter = intent::conjunction(conjuncts);
    79	        // The grant lane is the read as written: plan it exactly as the
    80	        // single-index plan would, so the binding check's fallback to it is
    81	        // that plan (an order-column pin a guard needs stays a pin, LLP 3000.036
    82	        // §16.3); its order must still match the party lanes', or no merge.
    83	        if grant { candidate.read(tables, &mut lane, false); } else { candidate.plan_lane(tables, &mut lane); }
    84	        if !grant && !decided(tables, &lane) { return None; }
    85	        // Every lane resumes from one shared position: the same order columns
    86	        // after its pins (lane planning never pins an order column).
    87	        if order.get_or_insert_with(|| lane.order_keys.clone()) != &lane.order_keys || lane.index.is_none() { return None; }
    88	        // An index holds at most 8 components; a lane never makes a program refuse.
    89	        if lane.keys.len() + lane.order_keys.len() > 8
    90	            || !candidate.branch_index_fits(&tables[&read.table], &lane) { return None; }
    91	        lanes.push(MergeLane { admission: read.filter.clone(), nulls: None, distinct: None, branch: Some(if grant { GRANT_LANE } else { BRANCH_LANE }.into()),
    92	            planned: Some(Box::new(lane)), project: None, table: None, prefix: Vec::new(), columns: None, loop_: None, site: read.site.clone() });
    93	    }
    94	    if candidate.error_count() != errors { return None; }
    95	    *requests = candidate;
    96	    Some(MergeExpr { view_order: Vec::new(), table: read.table.clone(), matching: None, filter: None, index: String::new(), order: range.order, take: range.take,
    97	        cursor: range.cursor.clone(), lanes, site: read.site.clone() })
    98	}

   100	/// An exact positive where witness that is itself a whole read-rule disjunct
   101	/// restricts W to that audience. Enumerate it directly; other audiences cannot
   102	/// contribute rows to W, including the system's rule bypass.
   103	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#17 — author-scoped pages retain their exact witness.
   104	fn scoped_membership(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {
   105	    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
   106	    let rule = read_rule(table)?;
   107	    let mut leaves = Vec::new(); disjuncts(rule, &mut leaves);
   108	    let mut predicates = Vec::new(); intent::split(*read.filter.clone()?, &mut predicates);
   109	    for (at, predicate) in predicates.iter().enumerate() {
   110	        if !matches!(predicate, Expr::Exists { .. }) || !leaves.iter().any(|leaf|
   111	            crate::print::expression(leaf) == crate::print::expression(predicate)) { continue; }
   112	        let rest: Vec<_> = predicates.iter().enumerate().filter(|(n, _)| *n != at).map(|(_, p)| p.clone()).collect();
   113	        if !rest.iter().all(total) { continue; }
   114	        let mut narrowed = read.clone(); narrowed.filter = intent::conjunction(rest);
   115	        let mut local = tables.clone();
   116	        local.get_mut(&read.table)?.decl.rules.iter_mut().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read)?.value = predicate.clone();
   117	        let mut candidate = requests.clone();
   118	        let mut merge = membership(&local, synthesized, &mut candidate, &narrowed)?;
   119	        // The system bypasses R, never W. Its whole-range descriptor retains
   120	        // the authored witness, and also binds all static target pins into
   121	        // the shared cursor scope. Other callers use exact discovery only.
   122	        for lane in &mut merge.lanes {
   123	            if lane.branch.as_deref() == Some(SYSTEM_LANE) {
   124	                let planned = lane.planned.as_mut()?;
   125	                let mut residual = planned.filter.take().map(|p| vec![*p]).unwrap_or_default();
   126	                residual.push(predicate.clone()); planned.filter = intent::conjunction(residual);
   127	            }
   128	            lane.admission = read.filter.clone();
   129	        }
   130	        *requests = candidate;
   131	        return Some(merge);
   132	    }
   133	    None
   134	}

   136	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — enumerate the decided membership source before merging its parent ranges.

   148	    let (mut parties, mut memberships) = (Vec::new(), Vec::new());
   149	    for disjunct in disjunct_list {
   150	        if let Some(column) = party(table, &disjunct) { if !parties.contains(&column) { parties.push(column); } }
   151	        else if matches!(disjunct, Expr::Exists { .. }) { memberships.push(disjunct); }
   152	        else { return None; }
   153	    }
   154	    if memberships.is_empty() { return None; }

   157	    // declined PAGE candidate, never an undistributed complete membership lane.

   161	        Some(branches) => branches,

   166	    // before the candidate can retain party/source indexes the original scan

   169	    if membership_targets.iter().any(|target| base.iter().any(|branch|
   170	        branch.iter().filter_map(|predicate| intent::equality(predicate, table)).any(|(column, _)| column == *target))) { return None; }
   171	    let descriptor_count = 1usize.saturating_add(base.len().saturating_mul(parties.len().saturating_add(memberships.len())));

   174	    let errors = candidate.error_count();
   175	    let mut lanes = Vec::with_capacity(descriptor_count);
   176	    let mut cap_repairs = Vec::new();
   177	    let mut order = None;
   178	    let mut system = read.clone(); candidate.read(tables, &mut system, false);
   179	    push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, system, SYSTEM_LANE, None, None, read.filter.clone())?;
   180	    for branch in &base {
   181	        for column in &parties {
   182	            let mut lane = read.clone();
   183	            let mut predicates = vec![viewer_pin(column, &read.site)]; predicates.extend(branch.iter().cloned());
   184	            lane.filter = intent::conjunction(predicates); candidate.plan_lane(tables, &mut lane);
   185	            if !decided(tables, &lane) { return None; }
   186	            push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, lane, BRANCH_LANE, None, None, read.filter.clone())?;
   187	        }

   189	            let mut source_read = read.clone();
   190	            let mut predicates = branch.clone(); predicates.push(predicate.clone()); source_read.filter = intent::conjunction(predicates);
   191	            let mut merge = super::intent_lanes::expand(tables, synthesized, &source_read)?;
   192	            let mut lane = merge.lanes.pop()?;
   193	            candidate.plan_lane(tables, lane.planned.as_mut()?);
   194	            let (_, source) = lane.loop_.as_mut()?;
   195	            let Expr::Read(source) = source else { return None };
   196	            let source_table = tables.get(&source.table)?;

   204	            // inferred index when the authored point supplies this proof.
   205	            let one_row = source_table.indexes.iter().filter(|index| source_table.total_unique(index)

   223	                    source.range = Some(RangeSpec { order: Order::Asc, take: 2, cursor: None, site: source.site.clone() });
   224	                }
   225	                source.keys = point; source.filter = intent::conjunction(residuals);
   226	                source.order_keys = vec!["id".into()]; source.index = Some(name);
   227	            } else { candidate = source_candidate; }
   228	            let source_index = candidate.branch_index(source_table, source)?;
   229	            let next_component = source.keys.len() < source_index.columns.len()
   230	                && source_index.columns.get(source.keys.len()) == Some(&source_column);
   231	            let one_row = source.keys.len() == source_index.columns.len() && source_table.total_unique(source_index);
   232	            if !next_component && !one_row { return None; }
   233	            // Uniform denial is not an empty discovery range. PAGE may commit
   234	            // its speculative indexes only when the fixed source pins prove
   235	            // a true read-rule disjunct, matching the core validator.
   236	            let admitted = one_row || membership_probe_total_unique(tables, synthesized, table, predicate)
   237	                || membership_source_capped(tables, source);
   238	            if !membership_source_witness(tables, synthesized, table, predicate, source)
   239	                || !source_read_admitted(source_table, source)
   240	                || candidate.branch_index_width(source_table, source).is_none_or(|width| width > 8)
   241	                || !candidate.branch_index_fits(source_table, source) { return None; }
   242	            if !admitted {
   243	                cap_repairs.push((source.table.clone(), membership_cap_repair(source_table, source, source_index)?));
   244	            }
   245	            push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, *lane.planned.take()?, MEMBERSHIP_LANE,
   246	                lane.loop_.take(), lane.distinct.take(), lane.admission.take())?;
   247	        }
   248	    }
   249	    if candidate.error_count() != errors { return None; }
   250	    if !cap_repairs.is_empty() {
   251	        cap_repairs.sort(); cap_repairs.dedup();
   252	        let declarations = cap_repairs.iter().map(|(table, declaration)| format!("`{declaration}` on table {table}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
   253	        requests.cap_repairs.insert(read.site.to_string(), format!("add {declarations}; this is a hard stored-row limit that can refuse adoption or later writes once the group is full, so add it only when it is a real product enrollment limit"));
   254	        return None;
   255	    }
   256	    *requests = candidate;
   257	    Some(MergeExpr { view_order: Vec::new(), table: read.table.clone(), matching: None, filter: None, index: String::new(), order: range.order, take: range.take,
   258	        cursor: range.cursor.clone(), lanes, site: read.site.clone() })
   259	}
   260	
   261	fn pinned_memberships(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String,String)>, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   262	    let Some(table) = tables.get(&read.table) else { return false };
   263	    let Some(rule) = read_rule(table) else { return false };
   264	    let mut conjuncts=Vec::new(); if let Some(filter)=&read.filter {intent::split(*filter.clone(),&mut conjuncts);}
   265	    let pins: Vec<_> = conjuncts.iter().filter_map(|term| intent::equality(term,table))
   266	        .filter(|(column,_)| !read.order_keys.contains(column)).map(|(column,_)|column).collect();
   267	    let mut leaves=Vec::new();disjuncts(rule,&mut leaves);
   268	    let members:Vec<_>=leaves.iter().filter(|leaf|membership_probe_point(tables,synthesized,table,leaf)).collect();
   269	    !members.is_empty() && members.iter().all(|leaf| membership_target(leaf).is_some_and(|target|pins.contains(&target)))
   270	}
   271	
   272	fn source_key_equality(key: KeyPart) -> Option<Expr> {
   273	    let column = key.column?;
   274	    let site = key.site;
   275	    Some(Expr::Binary { op: "=".into(), left: Box::new(Expr::Member {
   276	        base: Box::new(Expr::Focal { site: site.clone() }), name: column, site: site.clone(),
   277	    }), right: Box::new(key.value), site })
   278	}
   279	
   280	fn one_row_source_admitted(table: &TableInfo, source: &ReadExpr, index: &IndexDecl) -> bool {
   281	    let mut point = source.clone();
   282	    point.keys.retain(|key| key.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|column| index.columns.contains(column)));
   283	    source_read_admitted(table, &point)
   284	}
   285	
   286	fn source_read_admitted(table: &TableInfo, source: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   287	    table.decl.public.is_some() || read_rule(table).is_some_and(|rule| by_form(rule, source))
   288	}

   290	/// Confirm, while requests still live in the throwaway candidate, that every
   291	/// fixed probe component survived source planning as either a prefix pin or
   292	/// an exact necessary residual equality. The core repeats this check on bound
   293	/// IR; keeping it here prevents a later fallback from retaining unused indexes.
   294	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — membership candidates commit only after their complete witness is visible in discovery.
   295	fn membership_source_witness(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>,
   296	    focal: &TableInfo, predicate: &Expr, source: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   297	    let Expr::Exists { read: probe, .. } = predicate else { return false };
   298	    let Some(columns) = super::intent_lanes::probe_columns(tables, synthesized, focal, probe) else { return false };
   299	    probe.keys.iter().zip(columns).all(|(key, column)| intent::column(&key.value).is_some()
   300	        || source_fixed(source, &column, &key.value))
