   577	/// A branch merge's lanes may read different indexes of one table: each
   578	/// resumes on its own index from the shared merge position, so every lane
   579	/// needs scalar pins, a total position and the same position columns. A
   580	/// party or `where` lane must be decided by its prefix; a grant lane is
   581	/// decided per caller before it opens (`word_merge.rs`).
   582	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#3 — the binding check makes these hold; a lane that fails one keeps the eager traversal.
   583	pub fn branch_merge_lazy(schema: &Schema, merge: &Merge) -> bool {
   584	    let Some(first) = merge.lanes.first() else { return true };
   585	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&first.scan.table) else { return false };
   586	    let mut suffix: Option<(&crate::schema::Index, usize)> = None;
   587	    merge.lanes.iter().all(|lane| {
   588	        let scan = &lane.scan;
   589	        let Some(index) = table.index(&scan.index) else { return false };
   590	        let plain = index.components.iter().all(|component| component.column().is_some());
   591	        let total_unique = index.unique && plain && index.components.iter().all(|component| component.column()
   592	            .and_then(|column| table.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   593	        let prefix_total_unique = table.indexes.values().filter(|candidate| candidate.unique && candidate.is_plain())
   594	            .filter(|candidate| candidate.components.iter().all(|component| component.column()
   595	                .and_then(|column| table.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional())))
   596	            .any(|candidate| candidate.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().is_some_and(|column|
   597	                index.components.iter().take(scan.prefix.len()).any(|prefix| prefix.column() == Some(column)))));
   598	        // A membership target selected by its intrinsic id is physically a
   599	        // fully pinned byId point, but its logical merge position is that id.
   600	        // Compare it as the unpinned id suffix used by every other lane.
   601	        let position_prefix = scan.prefix.len() - usize::from(membership_point_id(schema, lane));
   602	        let same = match suffix {
   603	            Some((first_index, first_prefix)) => same_merge_position(table, first_index, first_prefix, index, position_prefix),
   604	            None => { suffix = Some((index, position_prefix)); true }
   605	        };
   606	        let authority = if lane.membership() { membership_branch(schema, lane) }
   607	            else if lane.system() { lane.source.is_none() }
   608	            else if lane.vacancy() { vacancy_branch(schema, lane) }
   609	            else if lane.grant() { lane.source.is_none() && covering_grant(schema, scan).is_some() }
   610	            else if lane.guarded() { lane.source.is_none() && page_guard_decision(schema, scan).is_some() }
   611	            else { lane.source.is_none() && scan_visibility(schema, scan).uniform };
   612	        lane.project.is_none() && lane.setup.is_empty() && scan.table == first.scan.table
   613	            && scan.prefix.iter().all(|key| matches!(key, IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(_)))
   614	            && scan.order == first.scan.order && !scan.existence && same
   615	            && (scan.prefix.len() < index.components.len() || prefix_total_unique || membership_point_id(schema, lane))
   616	            && index.word_component().is_none()
   617	            // A dynamic target-id lane uses `[id, order…]`: its scalar id
   618	            // prefix is already a total point even though the physical index
   619	            // is non-unique and need not repeat id as a suffix.
   620	            && plain && (total_unique || prefix_total_unique
   621	                || index.components.last().and_then(|component| component.column()).is_some_and(|c| c == "id"))
   622	            && authority
   623	    })
   624	}
   625	
   626	/// Exhaustion is observable only when the authored read rule itself contains
   627	/// a top-level absence check on an exact required point keyed by this row's
   628	/// intrinsic id. The lane stays private and evaluates the complete rule.
   629	fn vacancy_branch(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   630	    if lane.source.is_some() || lane.scan.filter.is_some() || !lane.scan.prefix.is_empty() { return false; }
   631	    let Some(outer) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
   632	    if outer.sync.is_some() { return false; }
   633	    outer.rules.read.disjuncts().iter().any(|rule| {
   634	        let RuleExpr::Not(inner) = rule else { return false };
   635	        let RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } = inner.as_ref() else { return false };
   636	        let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
   637	        let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
   638	        equalities.is_empty() && key == &[RuleTerm::Field("id".into())]
   639	            && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.len() == 1
   640	            && point.components[0].column().is_some_and(|column|
   641	                target.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
   642	    })
   643	}
   644	
   645	/// An intrinsic-id membership lane retains id as its logical merge position
   646	/// even though the physical byId scan pins that sole component.
   647	pub fn membership_point_id(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   648	    if !lane.membership() || lane.scan.range.is_some() { return false; }
   649	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
   650	    let Some(index) = table.index(&lane.scan.index) else { return false };
   651	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   652	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   653	    lane.scan.prefix.len() == index.components.len()
   654	        && index.components.last().and_then(|part| part.column()).is_some_and(|column| column == "id")
   655	        && membership_outer_column(schema, lane, source_column) == Some("id")
   656	}
   657	
   658	/// The source is readable under its fixed pins, and its row is the exact
   659	/// witness for the outer rule branch that the dynamic prefix opens. A cap on
   660	/// the fixed source group is one admission proof; a total point witness is
   661	/// another. The latter bounds each witness, while the shared read meter safely
   662	/// cuts the otherwise-unbounded inverted discovery before any row is emitted.
   663	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#15 — membership discovery proves each parent range without treating point uniqueness as a global membership cap.
   664	fn membership_branch(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   665	    membership_witness(schema, lane, false)
   666	}

   668	/// A null discovered value may open a target lane only when the matching

   671	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — fixed-id witnesses preserve nullable residual equality without treating nullable natural keys as points.
   672	pub(crate) fn membership_null_distinct(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   673	    membership_witness(schema, lane, true)
   674	}

   676	/// Whether PAGE v1's validator would admit this already-valid membership
   677	/// lane. Capability derivation uses the historical predicate exactly, so
   678	/// capped §14 plans remain v1-compatible while every newly admitted shape is
   679	/// fenced by `branch-membership-v2`.
   680	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#15 — PAGE2 capability negotiation preserves the v1 admission boundary.
   681	pub fn membership_v1(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   682	    if !lane.membership() { return false; }
   683	    let Some(source) = lane.source.as_ref().and_then(crate::ir::LaneSource::scan) else { return false };
   684	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   685	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(name) if name == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   686	    let Some(source_table) = schema.table(&source.table) else { return false };
   687	    let Some(source_index) = source_table.index(&source.index) else { return false };
   688	    let next_component = source.prefix.len() < source_index.components.len()
   689	        && source_index.components.get(source.prefix.len()).and_then(|part| part.column()) == Some(source_column);
   690	    let one_row_source = source.prefix.len() == source_index.components.len()
   691	        && source_index.unique && source_index.is_plain()
   692	        && source_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column))
   693	            .is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   694	    if source.existence || (!next_component && !one_row_source) || !scan_visibility(schema, source).decided_true { return false; }
   695	    let Bound::Literal(take @ 1..) = source.take else { return false };
   696	    let prefix_has = |field: &String| source_index.components.iter().take(source.prefix.len())
   697	        .any(|part| part.column() == Some(field));
   698	    let cap = source_table.constraints.iter().filter_map(|constraint| match constraint {
   699	        crate::schema::Constraint::Cap { by, max } if by.iter().all(prefix_has) && !source_table.cap_exempts_null(by) => Some(*max),
   700	        _ => None,
   701	    }).min();

   704	}
   705	
   706	fn membership_outer_column<'a>(schema: &'a Schema, lane: &'a crate::ir::MergeLane, source_column: &str) -> Option<&'a str> {
   707	    let table = schema.table(&lane.scan.table)?;
   708	    let index = table.index(&lane.scan.index)?;
   709	    let mut outer = None;
   710	    for (part, key) in index.components.iter().zip(&lane.scan.prefix) {
   711	        let (Some(column), Some(expr)) = (part.column(), key.scalar()) else { return None };
   712	        if matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) && name == source_column)
   713	            && outer.replace(column).is_some() { return None; }
   714	    }
   715	    outer.map(String::as_str)
   716	}
   717	
   718	fn membership_witness(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane, null_distinct: bool) -> bool {
   719	    let Some(source) = lane.source.as_ref().and_then(crate::ir::LaneSource::scan) else { return false };
   720	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   721	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(name) if name == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   722	    let Some(source_table) = schema.table(&source.table) else { return false };
   723	    let Some(source_index) = source_table.index(&source.index) else { return false };
   724	    let next_component = source.prefix.len() < source_index.components.len()
   725	        && source_index.components.get(source.prefix.len()).and_then(|part| part.column()) == Some(source_column);
   726	    // A fully pinned required unique identifies at most one source row, so
   727	    // any of that row's columns can be the discovered value. The source need
   728	    // not walk the distinct column as a following index component.
   729	    let one_row_source = source.prefix.len() == source_index.components.len()
   730	        && source_index.unique && source_index.is_plain()
   731	        && source_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column))
   732	            .is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   733	    if source.existence || (!next_component && !one_row_source)
   734	        || !scan_visibility(schema, source).decided_true { return false; }
   735	    // An applicable declaration can compile to a literal source bound. An
   736	    // exact total point may instead use the ordinary read meter: discovery
   737	    // remains fenced until that metered scan completes.
   738	    if !matches!(source.take, Bound::Meter | Bound::Literal(1..)) { return false; }
   739	    let prefix_has = |field: &String| source_index.components.iter().take(source.prefix.len())
   740	        .any(|part| part.column() == Some(field));
   741	    let capped_source_group = source_table.constraints.iter().any(|constraint| match constraint {
   742	        crate::schema::Constraint::Cap { by, max } if by.iter().all(prefix_has) && !source_table.cap_exempts_null(by) => Some(*max),
   743	        _ => None,
   744	    }.is_some());

   746	    let Some(outer_index) = outer_table.index(&lane.scan.index) else { return false };

   750	        if matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) && name == source_column)

   752	    }
   753	    let Some(outer_column) = outer_column else { return false };

   756	        .or_else(|| source.filter.as_deref().and_then(|filter| required_source_equality(filter, column)));

   758	    let pinned = |column: &str| pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)| (name.as_str() == column).then_some(*value));

   760	        let RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } = rule else { return false };
   761	        let Some(probe_index) = source_table.index(index) else { return false };
   762	        if table != &source.table || !probe_index.unique || !probe_index.is_plain() || probe_index.components.len() != key.len() { return false; }
   763	        let total_point = probe_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column()
   764	            .and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   765	        if !one_row_source && !capped_source_group && !total_point { return false; }
   766	        let matches = |column: &String, term: &RuleTerm| if column == source_column { matches!(term, RuleTerm::Field(field) if field == outer_column) }

   768	                // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#17 — an exact target pin fixes one component of a multi-column point witness.
   769	                RuleTerm::Field(field) => pinned(field).is_some_and(|pin| pin == expr),

   773	            || !equalities.iter().all(|(column, term)| matches(column, term)) { return false; }

   778	            fixed_rule_holds(schema, filter, rule, &lane.scan.table, outer_table, &pinned, &pins)))
   779	}
   780	
   781	/// Every sibling of a varying membership witness must already hold. Preserve
   782	/// authored Boolean grouping: every fixed conjunct is required, while proving
   783	/// one fixed alternative is enough for an authored disjunction.
   784	fn fixed_rule_holds<'a>(schema: &Schema, filter: &Expr, rule: &RuleExpr,
   785	    outer_name: &str, outer_table: &Table, pinned: &impl Fn(&str) -> Option<&'a Expr>,
   786	    pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
   787	    if rule_true(rule, outer_name, outer_table, pinned) { return true; }
   788	    match rule {
   789	        RuleExpr::And(parts) => parts.iter().all(|part|
   790	            fixed_rule_holds(schema, filter, part, outer_name, outer_table, pinned, pins)),
   791	        RuleExpr::Or(parts) => parts.iter().any(|part|
   792	            fixed_rule_holds(schema, filter, part, outer_name, outer_table, pinned, pins)),
   793	        _ => fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, filter, rule, pins),
   794	    }
   795	}
   796	
   797	/// A generated membership lane may retain prefix-fixed point grants
   798	/// as a residual predicate.  Recognize only the exact bound point expression:
   799	/// this proves that every row emitted by the lane satisfies that conjunct,
   800	/// while a missing, reordered or merely similar probe keeps the merge out.
   801	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — fixed grants may guard one varying per-row grant.
   802	fn fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema: &Schema, filter: &Expr, rule: &RuleExpr,
   803	    pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
   804	    if let Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::And, left, right } = filter {
   805	        return fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, left, rule, pins)
   806	            || fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, right, rule, pins);
   807	    }
   808	    // The compiler lowers a left-associated `a and b and c` into successive
   809	    // Let/If pairs. Accept only that exact dataflow chain: every right operand
   810	    // assigns the current result on its true branch, while `or` assigns on the
   811	    // false branch and any extra statement or mismatched name is rejected.
   812	    if let Expr::Block { body, value } = filter {
   813	        if body.is_empty() || body.len() % 2 != 0 { return false; }
   814	        let mut previous: Option<&String> = None;
   815	        let mut matched = false;
   816	        let mut at = 0;
   817	        while at < body.len() {
   818	            let [crate::ir::Stmt::Let { name, value: left }, crate::ir::Stmt::If {
   819	                condition: Expr::Variable(condition), then_body, else_body }] = &body[at..at + 2] else { return false };
   820	            let [crate::ir::Stmt::Assign { name: assigned, value: right }] = then_body.as_slice() else { return false };
   821	            if !else_body.is_empty() || condition != name || assigned != name { return false; }
   822	            if let Some(previous) = previous {
   823	                if !matches!(left, Expr::Variable(input) if input == previous) { return false; }
   824	            } else {
   825	                matched |= fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, left, rule, pins);
   826	            }
   827	            matched |= fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, right, rule, pins);
   828	            previous = Some(name);
   829	            at += 2;
   830	        }
   831	        if !matches!((value.as_ref(), previous), (Expr::Variable(result), Some(previous)) if result == previous) { return false; }
   832	        return matched;
   833	    }
   834	    let (Expr::Exists(read), RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. }) = (filter, rule) else { return false };
   835	    let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
   836	    let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
   837	    equalities.is_empty() && target.through.is_none() && target.not_before.is_none() && !target.is_expiring()
   838	        && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.iter().all(|part| part.column()
   839	            .and_then(|column| target.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
   840	        && &read.table == table && &read.index == index && read.key.len() == key.len()
   841	        && read.key.iter().zip(key).all(|(expression, term)| rule_term_expression(term, expression, pins))
   842	        && point_read_is_visible(schema, read)
   843	}
   844	
   845	/// A rule probe uses system authority, but the retained residual point uses
   846	/// ordinary caller authority. Re-prove that the generated, pin-substituted
   847	/// point is visible instead of asking about the unsubstituted authored terms.
   848	fn point_read_is_visible(schema: &Schema, read: &crate::ir::Read) -> bool {
   849	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&read.table) else { return false };
   850	    let Some(index) = table.index(&read.index) else { return false };
   851	    if !index.is_plain() || index.components.len() != read.key.len() { return false; }
   852	    let pinned = |name: &str| index.components.iter().zip(&read.key).find_map(|(part, value)|
   853	        (part.column().is_some_and(|column| column == name)).then_some(value));
   854	    visibility(&read.table, table, &table.rules.read, &pinned).decided_true
   855	}
   856	
   857	fn rule_term_expression(term: &RuleTerm, expression: &Expr, pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
   858	    match (term, expression) {
   859	        (RuleTerm::Viewer, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) => true,
   860	        (RuleTerm::Literal(value), Expr::Literal(actual)) => value == actual,
   861	        (RuleTerm::Field(field), expression) => pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)|
   862	            (name.as_str() == field).then_some(*value)).is_some_and(|value| value == expression),
   863	        _ => false,
   864	    }
   865	}
   866	
   867	/// A witness supplies one conjunct; every other conjunct must already hold
   868	/// by the lane's pins. Distribution does not drop a status/role condition.
   869	fn membership_rule(rule: &RuleExpr, witness: &impl Fn(&RuleExpr) -> bool, pinned: &impl Fn(&RuleExpr) -> bool) -> bool {
   870	    match rule {
   871	        RuleExpr::Or(parts) => parts.iter().any(|part| membership_rule(part, witness, pinned)),
   872	        RuleExpr::And(parts) => parts.iter().enumerate().any(|(at, part)|
   873	            membership_rule(part, witness, pinned) && parts.iter().enumerate().all(|(other, rule)| at == other || pinned(rule))),
   874	        _ => witness(rule),
   875	    }
   876	}
   877	
   878	/// A generated membership discovery filter may retain an exact fixed probe
   879	/// component after its capped prefix (`id = 'm1'`, for example). Only a bare
   880	/// equality in the filter's conjunction is a necessary fact about every
   881	/// discovered row; alternatives and other implications remain unsupported.
   882	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — fixed membership witness columns may be proved by exact source residuals.
   883	fn required_source_equality<'a>(filter: &'a Expr, column: &str) -> Option<&'a Expr> {
   884	    match filter {
   885	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::And, left, right } =>
   886	            required_source_equality(left, column).or_else(|| required_source_equality(right, column)),
   887	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } if source_field(left, column) => Some(right),
   888	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } if source_field(right, column) => Some(left),
   889	        _ => None,
   890	    }
   891	}
   892	
   893	fn source_field(expr: &Expr, column: &str) -> bool {
   894	    matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name }
   895	        if name == column && matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == "$filter"))
   896	}
   897	
   898	fn term_matches_expr(term: &RuleTerm, expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   899	    match (term, expr) { (RuleTerm::Viewer, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) => true,
   900	        (RuleTerm::Literal(value), expr) => literal_matches_expr(value, expr), _ => false }
   901	}
   902	
   903	fn literal_matches_expr(value: &Value, expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   904	    match expr {
   905	        Expr::Literal(other) => value == other,
   906	        Expr::Unary { op: crate::ir::UnaryOp::Neg, .. } => matches!(value, Value::Int(expected)
   907	            if expr_int_literal(expr).is_some_and(|actual| actual == i128::from(*expected))),
   908	        _ => false,
   909	    }
   910	}

   952	/// An authored equality may already be the right authorization scope while a

   956	fn equality_filter_index_rewrite(catalog: &Catalog, table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table,
   957	    scan: &snapback4_core::ir::Scan, unpinned: &[String], leading: &[String],
   958	    declaration_site: &crate::Site, read_site: Option<&crate::Site>) -> Option<String> {
   959	    use snapback4_core::ir::{BinaryOp, Expr};
   960	    fn pins(expr: &Expr, column: &str) -> bool {

   962	            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left, right } => pins(left, column) || pins(right, column),
   963	            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } => field(left, column) && independent(right)
   964	                || field(right, column) && independent(left),
   965	            _ => false,
   966	        }
   967	    }
   968	    fn field(expr: &Expr, column: &str) -> bool {

   971	    }
   972	    fn independent(expr: &Expr) -> bool {

   975	            Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => independent(left) && independent(right),
   976	            Expr::Unary { value, .. } => independent(value),
   977	            Expr::Array(values) => values.iter().all(independent),
   978	            Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().all(independent),
   979	            Expr::Builtin { args, .. } => args.iter().all(independent),
   980	            Expr::Get(_) | Expr::Exists(_) | Expr::Scan(_) | Expr::Merge(_) | Expr::Block { .. } => false,
   981	            _ => true,
   982	        }
   983	    }

   987	        .map(|first| first.to_uppercase().collect::<String>() + chars.as_str()).unwrap_or_default() };

   990	    let mut name = format!("by{stem}");

  1001	    // an earlier, unrelated read and print a query that does not compile.
  1002	    let read_site = read_site?;
  1003	    let (mut read, text, grouped, start) = catalog.source.read(read_site)?;
  1004	    // Positional/named bracket keys address the old index's columns. Merely
  1005	    // prepending authorization components would retarget those values, so a
  1006	    // coherent repair needs authored key expressions for the new prefix.
  1007	    if !read.keys.is_empty() { return None; }
  1008	    if read.index.as_deref() != Some(scan.index.as_str()) { return None; }
  1009	    read.index = Some(name.clone());
  1010	    let fixed = crate::ast::Expr::Read(read);
  1011	    let replacement = crate::print::with_source_at(&fixed, if grouped { 8 } else { 0 }, &|expr| catalog.source.get(expr));
  1012	    let declaration = catalog.source.declarations.get(&declaration_site.to_string())?;
  1013	    let line = start.line.checked_sub(declaration_site.line)? as usize;
  1014	    let lines = declaration.split('\n').collect::<Vec<_>>();
  1015	    let column = start.col.checked_sub(1)? as usize;
  1016	    let byte = lines.get(line)?.char_indices().map(|(i, _)| i).chain([lines[line].len()]).nth(column)?;
  1017	    let offset = lines[..line].iter().map(|line| line.len() + 1).sum::<usize>() + byte;
  1018	    let span = offset..offset.checked_add(text.len())?;
  1019	    if declaration.get(span.clone())? != text { return None; }
  1020	    let rewritten = crate::parser::repairs::splice(declaration, vec![(span, replacement)])?;
  1021	    Some(format!("on table {} add `by {name}: {}`; use the same index in the rewritten query:\n```quarry\n{rewritten}\n```", scan.table, leading.join(", ")))
  1022	}
  1023	
  1024	fn matching_merge_rewrite(schema: &Schema, scan: &snapback4_core::ir::Scan, table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table) -> Option<String> {
  1025	    use snapback4_core::{ir::IndexKeyExpr, schema::{RuleExpr, RuleTerm}};
  1026	    if !scan.prefix.iter().any(|key| matches!(key, IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(_))) { return None; }
  1027	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — a membership conjunct supplies lanes when their scope decides the whole rule.
  1028	    fn conjuncts(rule: &RuleExpr) -> Vec<&RuleExpr> {
  1029	        match rule { RuleExpr::And(terms) => terms.iter().flat_map(conjuncts).collect(), other => vec![other] }
  1030	    }
  1031	    conjuncts(&table.rules.read).into_iter().find_map(|rule| {
  1032	        let RuleExpr::Exists { table: members, index: membership_index, key, .. } = rule else { return None; };
  1033	        let membership = schema.table(members)?;
  1034	        let columns = membership.index(membership_index)?.columns();
  1035	        let principal = key.iter().position(|term| matches!(term, RuleTerm::Viewer))?;
  1036	        let scope: Vec<_> = key.iter().filter_map(|term| if let RuleTerm::Field(field) = term { Some(field.clone()) } else { None }).collect();
  1037	        let deciding = snapback4_core::engine::rules::deciding_fields(&scan.table, table, &table.rules.read);
  1038	        if !deciding.iter().all(|field| scope.contains(field)) { return None; }
  1039	        let fields = table.index(&scan.index)?.word_component()?.1;
  1040	        let (_, words_index) = table.indexes.iter().find(|(_, index)| index.word_component().is_some_and(|(position, text)| index.columns()[..position] == scope && text == fields))?;
  1041	        let _ = membership.indexes.iter().find(|(_, index)| index.is_plain() && index.columns().first() == columns.get(principal) && index.columns().last().is_some_and(|column| column == "id"))?;
  1042	        let order = words_index.columns().into_iter().skip(scope.len()).map(|column| format!(".{column}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
  1043	        Some(format!("{} matching q where {} last {} by {order} after c — one verdict per scope; complete while every head opens and the page fits in one read; or read the page on the device", scan.table, table.rules.read.quarry(), scan.take.maximum()))
  1044	    })
  1045	}

   497	/// The bounded positive alternatives of the read's top-level conjunction.
   498	/// Each `or` contributes a Cartesian factor, so
   499	/// `(a = x or b = x) and (state = 'open' or state = 'held')` becomes four
   500	/// independently pinned lanes.  Keep the published eight-lane ceiling while
   501	/// allowing an exact query predicate to narrow a wider read-rule union.
   502	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — exact positive query scopes may combine party and status alternatives.
   503	fn where_branches(table: &TableInfo, conjuncts: &[Expr]) -> Option<Vec<Vec<Expr>>> {
   504	    let mut branches = vec![Vec::new()];
   505	    let mut distributed = false;
   506	    for conjunct in conjuncts {
   507	        let mut alternatives = Vec::new();
   508	        disjuncts(conjunct, &mut alternatives);
   509	        distributed |= alternatives.len() > 1;
   510	        if branches.len().saturating_mul(alternatives.len()) > MAX_BRANCHES { return None; }
   511	        branches = branches.into_iter().flat_map(|prefix| alternatives.iter().map(move |alternative| {
   512	            let mut lane = prefix.clone();
   513	            intent::split(alternative.clone(), &mut lane);
   514	            lane
   515	        })).collect();
   516	    }
   517	    if !distributed || branches.len() < 2 || branches.iter().any(|lane| !lane.iter().any(|conjunct| pin(table, conjunct))) {

   519	    }
   520	    Some(branches)
   521	}

   523	/// An equality the planner turns into an index prefix. A nullable column is
   524	/// pinned only to `viewer`, never to a value that may be null, so a branch
   525	/// never seeks a null the original filter rejects.
   526	fn pin(table: &TableInfo, conjunct: &Expr) -> bool {
   527	    let Some((column, value)) = intent::equality(conjunct, table) else { return false };
   528	    column != "id" && table.columns.get(&column).is_some_and(|ty| !ty.optional || is_viewer(&value))
   529	}
   530	
   531	/// The planned read's prefix decides the read rule: every column the rule
   532	/// reads is pinned (with the self-witness proof), or the rule is true by the
   533	/// pins' form (the core's `rule_true`, and the Lean model's `Rule.byForm`).
   534	pub(super) fn decided(tables: &Tables, planned: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   535	    let Some(table) = tables.get(&planned.table) else { return false };
   536	    if super::intent_lanes::prefix_decides_rule(tables, planned) { return true; }
   537	    read_rule(table).is_some_and(|rule| by_form(rule, planned))
   538	}
   539	
   540	/// `allow`, `.col = viewer` pinned to `viewer`, `.col = 'x'` pinned to `'x'`,
   541	/// and `or`/`and` of those.
   542	pub(super) fn by_form(rule: &Expr, planned: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   543	    let pinned = |column: &str| planned.keys.iter().find(|key| key.column.as_deref() == Some(column)).map(|key| &key.value);
   544	    match rule {
   545	        Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "allow" => true,
   546	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => by_form(left, planned) || by_form(right, planned),
   547	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => by_form(left, planned) && by_form(right, planned),
   548	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" => {
   549	            let (column, value) = match (intent::column(left), intent::column(right)) {
   550	                (Some(column), None) => (column, right.as_ref()),
   551	                (None, Some(column)) => (column, left.as_ref()),
   552	                _ => return false,
   553	            };
   554	            match (value, pinned(column)) {
   555	                (value, Some(pin)) if is_viewer(value) => is_viewer(pin),
   556	                (Expr::Literal { value, .. }, Some(Expr::Literal { value: pin, .. })) => value == pin && *value != Literal::Null,
   557	                _ => false,
   558	            }
   559	        }
   560	        _ => false,
   561	    }
   562	}

   564	pub(super) fn read_rule(table: &TableInfo) -> Option<&Expr> {
   565	    match table.decl.rules.iter().filter(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read).collect::<Vec<_>>().as_slice() {
   566	        [rule] => Some(&rule.value),
   567	        _ => None,
   568	    }
   569	}
   570	
   571	fn disjuncts(value: &Expr, out: &mut Vec<Expr>) {
   572	    if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value && op == "or" { disjuncts(left, out); disjuncts(right, out); }
   573	    else { out.push(value.clone()); }
   574	}
   575	
   576	/// `.col = viewer` (either side) on a principal column of the row.
   577	fn party(table: &TableInfo, value: &Expr) -> Option<String> {
   578	    let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value else { return None };
   579	    if op != "=" { return None; }
   580	    let column = match (intent::column(left), intent::column(right)) {
   581	        (Some(column), None) if is_viewer(right) => column,
   582	        (None, Some(column)) if is_viewer(left) => column,
   583	        _ => return None,
   584	    };
   585	    (table.columns.get(column)?.kind == TypeKind::Principal).then(|| column.to_owned())
   586	}
   587	
   588	/// A rule disjunct every row column of which the page pins: none for a role
   589	/// or a seat, the parent's column for a parent probe under its pin.
   590	pub(super) fn columns_within(value: &Expr, pinned: &[String]) -> bool {
   591	    let mut copy = value.clone();
   592	    let mut within = true;
   593	    super::intent_walk::expression(&mut copy, &mut |value| {
   594	        if let Expr::Member { base, name, .. } = value && matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) && !pinned.contains(name) { within = false; }
   595	        else if let Expr::Focal { .. } = value { /* reached through a member, checked there */ }
   596	    });
   597	    within && !has_focal_without_member(value)
   598	}
   599	
   600	/// A bare `.` (the whole row) is not a pinned column.
   601	fn has_focal_without_member(value: &Expr) -> bool {
   602	    match value {
   603	        Expr::Focal { .. } => true,
   604	        Expr::Member { base, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) => false,
   605	        Expr::Member { base, .. } | Expr::Unary { value: base, .. } => has_focal_without_member(base),
   606	        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => has_focal_without_member(left) || has_focal_without_member(right),
   607	        Expr::Exists { read, .. } => read.keys.iter().any(|key| has_focal_without_member(&key.value)) || read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(has_focal_without_member),
   608	        Expr::Call { args, .. } | Expr::Array { items: args, .. } => args.iter().any(has_focal_without_member),
   609	        _ => false,
   610	    }
   611	}
   612	
   613	/// A predicate whose evaluation never refuses and never reads: comparisons,
   614	/// `in` and boolean operators over the row's own columns, arguments, literals
   615	/// and `viewer`. Arithmetic (even `now - 7d`, which overflows at the clock's
   616	/// bound), assertions, calls and reads are left to the single-index plan, and
   617	/// so is a member of anything but the row (`.meta.ok`, `m.ok`), which binds to
   618	/// a read of the referenced row that a meter can cut (GPT round 4, finding 1).
   619	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#2 — totality: no read, so a lane is cut only between candidates
   620	pub(super) fn total(value: &Expr) -> bool {
   621	    match value {
   622	        Expr::Literal { .. } | Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } => true,
   623	        Expr::Member { base, .. } => matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }),
   624	        Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } => op == "not" && total(value),
   625	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=" | "<" | "<=" | ">" | ">=" | "and" | "or" | "in") && total(left) && total(right),
   626	        Expr::Array { items, .. } => items.iter().all(total),
   627	        _ => false,
   628	    }
   629	}
   630	
   631	fn is_viewer(value: &Expr) -> bool { matches!(value, Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer") }
   632	
   633	fn viewer_pin(column: &str, site: &Site) -> Expr {
   634	    Expr::Binary { op: "=".into(), site: site.clone(),
   635	        left: Box::new(Expr::Member { base: Box::new(Expr::Focal { site: site.clone() }), name: column.into(), site: site.clone() }),
   636	        right: Box::new(Expr::Name { name: "viewer".into(), site: site.clone() }) }
   637	}

     1	//! Physical page eligibility derived solely from Quarry surface declarations.
     2	use super::*;
     3	
     4	enum PagePins {
     5	    Plain(Vec<String>),
     6	    Product { system: Vec<String>, branches: Vec<Vec<String>> },
     7	    Invalid,
     8	}
     9	
    10	/// Reconstruct the native planner's bounded Cartesian product from the
    11	/// authored filter.  This stays surface-derived: the executable model cannot
    12	/// trust the binder's proof metadata when deciding whether comparison is safe.
    13	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — every modeled PAGE filter lane has its own exact physical prefix.
    14	fn page_pins(table: &Table, read: &ReadExpr) -> PagePins {
    15	    let Some(filter) = read.filter.as_deref() else { return PagePins::Plain(Vec::new()) };
    16	    let mut conjuncts = Vec::new();
    17	    flatten_and(filter, &mut conjuncts);
    18	    let system = model_pin_columns(table, &conjuncts, &read.order_keys);
    19	    let mut branches = vec![Vec::new()];
    20	    let mut distributed = false;
    21	    for conjunct in &conjuncts {
    22	        let mut alternatives = Vec::new();
    23	        flatten_or(conjunct, &mut alternatives);
    24	        distributed |= alternatives.len() > 1;
    25	        if branches.len().saturating_mul(alternatives.len()) > 8 { return PagePins::Invalid; }
    26	        branches = branches.into_iter().flat_map(|prefix| alternatives.iter().map(move |alternative| {
    27	            let mut lane = prefix.clone();
    28	            flatten_and(alternative, &mut lane);
    29	            lane
    30	        })).collect();
    31	    }
    32	    if !distributed || branches.len() < 2 { return PagePins::Plain(system); }
    33	    if branches.iter().any(|branch| !branch.iter().any(|atom| page_pin(table, atom))) {
    34	        return PagePins::Invalid;
    35	    }
    36	    PagePins::Product {
    37	        system,
    38	        branches: branches.into_iter().map(|branch| model_pin_columns(table, &branch, &read.order_keys)).collect(),
    39	    }
    40	}
    41	
    42	fn page_pin(table: &Table, expression: &Expr) -> bool {
    43	    if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = expression && op == "=" {
    44	        let (column, value) = if let Some(column) = column_of(left) { (column, right.as_ref()) }
    45	            else if let Some(column) = column_of(right) { (column, left.as_ref()) }
    46	            else { return false };
    47	        return column != "id" && model_independent(value) && table.cols.iter().find(|field| field.name == column)
    48	            .is_some_and(|field| !field.nullable || matches!(value, Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer"));
    49	    }
    50	    let value = if let Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } = expression && op == "not" { value.as_ref() } else { expression };
    51	    let Some(column) = column_of(value) else { return false };
    52	    column != "id" && table.cols.iter().find(|field| field.name == column)
    53	        .is_some_and(|field| !field.nullable && matches!(field.kind, Kind::Bool))
    54	}
    55	
    56	/// The executable predicate classifier currently knows literal/viewer point
    57	/// terms only. Preserve an explicit boundary for admitted native argument
    58	/// and signed-expression keys rather than reporting a model disagreement
    59	/// under an incompletely translated completeness proof.
    60	pub(super) fn static_key_issue(read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
    61	    fn missing(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
    62	        match expr {
    63	            Expr::Exists { read, .. } => read.keys.iter().all(|key| model_independent(&key.value))
    64	                && read.keys.iter().any(|key|
    65	                !matches!(&key.value, Expr::Literal { .. })
    66	                && !matches!(&key.value, Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer")),
    67	            Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => missing(left) || missing(right),
    68	            Expr::Unary { value, .. } => missing(value),
    69	            _ => false,
    70	        }
    71	    }
    72	    read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(missing).then_some("page-static-key-expression")
    73	}
    74	
    75	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#16 — reproduce implicit bounds from surface
    76	// constraints, independently of the planner's selected index or IR.
    77	pub(super) fn implicit_page(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<ReadExpr> {
    78	    if read.range.is_some() || !read.keys.is_empty() || read.index.is_some() { return None; }
    79	    let target = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    80	    let mut predicates = Vec::new();
    81	    if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { flatten_and(filter, &mut predicates); }
    82	    let pinned: Vec<String> = predicates.iter().filter_map(|predicate| model_equality_column(target, predicate)).collect();
    83	    let cap = target.caps.iter().filter(|(_, by)| by.iter().all(|column| pinned.contains(column)
    84	        && !target.cols.iter().any(|col| &col.name == column && col.on_delete == Some("setNull"))))
    85	        .map(|(max, _)| *max).chain(target.uniques.iter().filter(|key| key.iter().all(|column|
    86	            pinned.contains(column) && (column == "id" || target.cols.iter().any(|col| &col.name == column && !col.nullable))))
    87	            .map(|_| 1)).min()?;
    88	    if cap > 8192 { return None; }
    89	    let mut page = read.clone();
    90	    page.range = Some(ast::RangeSpec { order: Order::Asc, take: cap, cursor: None, site: read.site.clone() });
    91	    Some(page)
    92	}

    94	pub(super) fn membership_physical_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
    95	    if read.range.is_none() || !read.keys.is_empty() { return None }
    96	    let target = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    97	    let decl = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    98	    let rule = decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read && rule.bindings.is_empty()).map(|rule| &rule.value)?;
    99	    let mut leaves = Vec::new();
   100	    flatten_or(rule, &mut leaves);
   101	    let mut parties = Vec::new();
   102	    let mut memberships = Vec::new();
   103	    for leaf in leaves {
   104	        if let Some(column) = party_column(target, leaf) {
   105	            if !parties.iter().any(|party| party == column) { parties.push(column.to_owned()); }
   106	        }
   107	        else if let Some(membership) = membership_physical(module, target, leaf) { memberships.push(membership); }
   108	        else { return None; }
   109	    }
   110	    if memberships.is_empty() { return None }
   111	    if memberships.iter().any(|membership| !membership.source_admissible) {
   112	        return Some("page-membership-source-unique");
   113	    }
   114	    let mut fits = Vec::new();
   115	    for membership in &memberships {
   116	        let Some(table) = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == membership.source) else { return Some("page-membership-key-bound") };
   117	        fits.push(match &membership.index {
   118	            Some(index) => model_selected_index_fit(module, table, index),
   119	            None => model_plain_index_fit(module, table, &membership.columns, false),
   120	        });
   121	    }
   122	    let (system_pins, branches) = match page_pins(target, read) {
   123	        PagePins::Plain(system) => (system.clone(), vec![system]),
   124	        PagePins::Product { system, branches } => (system, branches),
   125	        PagePins::Invalid => return Some("page-membership-filter-product"),
   126	    };
   127	    if 1usize.saturating_add(branches.len().saturating_mul(parties.len().saturating_add(memberships.len()))) > 8 {
   128	        return Some("page-membership-filter-product");
   129	    }
   130	    fits.push(model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(system_pins, &read.order_keys), true));
   131	    for branch in branches {
   132	        for prefix in parties.iter().chain(memberships.iter().map(|membership| &membership.target_parent)) {
   133	            let mut pins = branch.clone();
   134	            if !pins.contains(prefix) { pins.push(prefix.clone()); pins.sort(); }
   135	            fits.push(model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(pins, &read.order_keys), true));
   136	        }
   137	    }
   138	    if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Ambiguous) { Some("page-membership-index-selection") }
   139	    else if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Nonportable) { Some("page-membership-key-bound") }
   140	    else { None }
   141	}
   142	
   143	/// Compound PAGE3 remains outside the executable model when its physical
   144	/// lanes cannot be independently reconstructed from the surface. Logical
   145	/// distribution is proved separately; an unsupported read is never a pass.
   146	pub(super) fn compound_physical_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
   147	    if read.range.is_none() || !read.keys.is_empty() { return None; }
   148	    let target = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
   149	    let decl = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
   150	    let rule = decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read)?.value.clone();
   151	    fn compound(e: &Expr) -> bool {
   152	        match e {
   153	            Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "and" => true,
   154	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => compound(left) || compound(right),
   155	            _ => false,
   156	        }
   157	    }
   158	    if !compound(&rule) && !matches!(&rule, Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "or") { return None; }
   159	    fn ranges(e: &Expr) -> Option<Vec<Vec<&Expr>>> {
   160	        let out = match e {
   161	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => {
   162	                let mut out = ranges(left)?; out.extend(ranges(right)?); out
   163	            }
   164	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => {
   165	                let a = ranges(left)?; let b = ranges(right)?;
   166	                if a.len().saturating_mul(b.len()) > 7 { return None; }

   169	            other => vec![vec![other]],
   170	        };
   171	        (out.len() <= 7).then_some(out)
   172	    }
   173	    let mut simple = Vec::new(); flatten_or(&rule, &mut simple);
   174	    // Existing party and exact point-membership classifiers take precedence;
   175	    // their where alternatives already have an independent physical proof.
   176	    if simple.iter().all(|leaf| party_column(target, leaf).is_some() || membership_physical(module, target, leaf).is_some()) { return None; }
   177	    let leaves = ranges(&rule)?;
   178	    let system = match page_pins(target, read) {
   179	        PagePins::Plain(system) => system,
   180	        PagePins::Product { .. } | PagePins::Invalid => return Some("page-compound-where-branches"),
   181	    };
   182	    // The model's compound classifier deliberately keeps the where-branch
   183	    // composition outside its fragment until it models each source prefix.
   184	    let mut fits = vec![model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(system.clone(), &read.order_keys), true)];
   185	    for leaf in leaves {
   186	        let mut pins = system.clone();
   187	        let mut predicates = Vec::new();
   188	        if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { flatten_and(filter, &mut predicates); }
   189	        predicates.extend(leaf.iter().copied());

   191	            let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = atom else { return None };
   192	            if op != "=" { return None; }
   193	            let (column, value) = if let Some(column) = column_of(left) { (column, right.as_ref()) }
   194	                else { (column_of(right)?, left.as_ref()) };
   195	            matches!(value, Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. }).then(|| (column.to_owned(), value.clone()))
   196	        }).collect();

   198	            if let Some(column) = model_equality_column(target, atom) {
   199	                if target.cols.iter().any(|col| col.name == column && col.nullable)
   200	                    && party_column(target, atom).is_none() { return Some("page-compound-nullable-pin"); }
   201	                pins.push(column);
   202	            }
   203	        }
   204	        for atom in leaf {
   205	            let mut substituted = atom.clone();
   206	            crate::bind::intent_walk::expression(&mut substituted, &mut |e| {
   207	                if let Some(value) = column_of(e).and_then(|column| fixed.get(column)) { *e = value.clone(); }
   208	            });
   209	            if let Some(membership) = membership_physical(module, target, &substituted) {
   210	                if !pins.contains(&membership.target_parent) && !system.contains(&membership.target_parent) {
   211	                    if !membership.source_admissible { return Some("page-membership-source-unique"); }
   212	                    pins.push(membership.target_parent);
   213	                    let source = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == membership.source)?;
   214	                    fits.push(match &membership.index {
   215	                        Some(index) => model_selected_index_fit(module, source, index),
   216	                        None => model_plain_index_fit(module, source, &membership.columns, false),
   217	                    });
   218	                }
   219	            }
   220	        }
   221	        pins.sort(); pins.dedup();
   222	        fits.push(model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(pins, &read.order_keys), true));
   223	    }
   224	    if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Ambiguous) { Some("page-compound-index-selection") }
   225	    else if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Nonportable) { Some("page-compound-key-bound") }
   226	    else { None }
   227	}
   228	
   229	
   230	/// The model's direct grant theorem remains positive. Standard bounded
   231	/// negative-grant pages use the independent branch classifier; direct scalar
   232	/// absence/aggregation and fallible-filter fallback stay explicitly outside
   233	/// that executable completeness fragment until its negative-view proof lands.
   234	pub(super) fn negative_grant_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
   235	    if !read.keys.is_empty() && read.filter.is_none() && read.range.is_none() { return None; }
   236	    fn negative_probe(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   237	        match expr {
   238	            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op=="not" => matches!(value.as_ref(),Expr::Exists {..}),
   239	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op=="and" || op=="or" => negative_probe(left) || negative_probe(right),
   240	            _=>false,
   241	        }
   242	    }
   243	    let table = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
   244	    let rule = &table.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read)?.value;
   245	    if !negative_probe(rule) { return None; }
   246	    fn total(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   247	        match expr {
   248	            Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } => true,
   249	            Expr::Member { base, .. } => matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }),
   250	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=" | "<" | "<=" | ">" | ">=" | "and" | "or" | "in") && total(left) && total(right),
   251	            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } => op == "not" && total(value),
   252	            Expr::Array { items, .. } => items.iter().all(total), _ => false,
   253	        }
   254	    }
   255	    fn distributed_width(expr: &Expr) -> usize {
   256	        match expr {
   257	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op=="or" => distributed_width(left).saturating_add(distributed_width(right)).min(8),
   258	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op=="and" => distributed_width(left).saturating_mul(distributed_width(right)).min(8),
   259	            _=>1,
   260	        }
   261	    }
   262	    (read.range.is_none() || read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| !total(filter)) || distributed_width(rule) >= 8)
   263	        .then_some("page-negative-grant-fallback")
   264	}
   265	
   266	/// Named order normalization is speculative in the native compiler. Only
   267	/// the independently modeled unfiltered mixed range fragment normalizes here;
   268	/// filtered or one-party fallback candidates stay named Unsupported.
   269	pub(super) fn named_merge_fragment(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   270	    if read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| !matches!(filter, Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(true), .. })) { return false; }
   271	    let Some(target)=module.tables.iter().find(|t|t.name==read.table) else {return false;};
   272	    let Some(rule)=module.surface_tables.iter().find(|t|t.name==read.table).and_then(|t|t.rules.iter().find(|r|r.effect==Effect::Read)).map(|r|&r.value) else {return false;};
   273	    if !matches!(rule,Expr::Binary {op,..} if op=="or") {return false;}
   274	    let mut leaves=Vec::new();flatten_or(rule,&mut leaves);
   275	    leaves.len()<=7 && leaves.iter().any(|leaf|party_column(target,leaf).is_some())
   276	        && leaves.iter().all(|leaf|party_column(target,leaf).is_some() || membership_physical(module,target,leaf).is_some()
   277	            || model_equality_column(target,leaf).is_some())
   278	}

+
+/// A shared equality supplies the physical prefix for every Cartesian lane;
+/// the alternative factors themselves may remain total residual comparisons.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — common positive pins count in every product branch.
+pub fn page_membership_shared_filter_product(seed: u64) -> Case {
+    let mut case = base(seed);
+    case.program.tables[0].rule = Rule::Witness;
+    case.program.tables[0].effects = None;
+    case.program.tables[0].residency = Residency::Online;
+    case.program.tables[0].columns.push(column("region", Type::Text));
+    case.program.tables[0].columns.push(column("rank", Type::Int));
+    let access = case.program.tables.iter_mut().find(|table| table.name == "access").unwrap();
+    access.rule = Rule::Allow; access.effects = None; access.residency = Residency::Public;
+    access.expire = None; access.not_before = None;
+    case.program.body = Body::Read { kind: Read::Page, take: 1, descending: seed % 2 == 0,
+        filter: false, owner: false, project: None };
+    case.program.domain = Some(".region = 'north'".into());
+    case.program.residual = Some("(.score > 0 or .score > 10) and (.rank > 0 or .rank > 10)".into());
+    case.program.order = Some(vec!["label".into(), "score".into()]);
+    case.program.named_page_order = None; case.program.prelude.clear(); case.program.lets.clear(); case.steps.clear();
+    for (table, row) in &mut case.rows { if table == "items" {
+        row["region"] = json!(if seed % 3 == 0 { "south" } else { "north" });
+        row["rank"] = json!((seed % 15) as i64);
+    }}
+    case
+}

@@ -796,3 +796,33 @@ fn membership_page_with_two_equality_alternative_factors_is_complete_in_lean_and

 }
+
+#[test]
+fn page5_review_boundaries_agree_in_generated_native_and_lean_cases() {
+    let have_model = model_built();
+    for seed in 0..4 {
+        for (case, complete, model_boundary) in [
+            (snapback4_difftest::generate::page_membership_filter_overflow(seed), false, Some("page-membership-filter-product")),
+            (snapback4_difftest::generate::page_membership_shared_filter_product(seed), true, None),
+            (snapback4_difftest::generate::page5_implicit_id_vacancy(seed), true, None),
+        ] {
+            let source = case.program.source();
+            execute::compile(&source).unwrap_or_else(|error| panic!("seed {seed}: {error}\n{source}"));
+            let mut engine = execute::engine(&case, &source).unwrap();
+            let outcome = execute::execute(&mut engine, &case, "run", &case.args);
+            assert_eq!(outcome.value["complete"], complete, "seed {seed}: {outcome:?}\n{source}");
+            let mut report = Report::default(); campaign::check(&case, &mut report, |_| {});
+            assert!(!report.failed(), "seed {seed}: {report:?}\n{source}");
+            if have_model {
+                let checked = snapback4_difftest::lean::compare(&case, &source).unwrap();
+                if let Some(reason) = model_boundary {
+                    assert!(matches!(&checked.verdict, Verdict::Unsupported(actual) if actual == reason),
+                        "seed {seed}: {:?}\n{source}", checked.verdict);
+                    assert_eq!(checked.executed, 0, "seed {seed}: model boundary executed\n{source}");
+                } else {
+                    assert!(matches!(checked.verdict, Verdict::Pass), "seed {seed}: {:?}\n{source}", checked.verdict);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

+fn rule_term_expression(term: &RuleTerm, expression: &Expr, pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
+    match (term, expression) {
+        (RuleTerm::Viewer, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) => true,
+        (RuleTerm::Literal(value), Expr::Literal(actual)) => value == actual,
+        (RuleTerm::Field(field), expression) => pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)|
+            (name.as_str() == field).then_some(*value)).is_some_and(|value| value == expression),
+        _ => false,
+    }
 }

   105	    /// The page and its continuation candidate (`None` when it ends).
   106	    fn merge_branch_lanes(&mut self, merge: &Merge, prefixes: Vec<Vec<Value>>, take: usize, after: Option<(Vec<Value>, bool)>) -> Result<(Vec<Value>, bool, Option<Vec<Value>>), RuntimeError> {
   107	        let order = merge.lanes[0].scan.order;
   108	        let site = merge.site.as_str();

   111	        // finishes before any target row may be emitted.

   224	                if metered_merge { self.meter.heap(DisclosedKeys(&head.key).heap())?; }
   225	                last = Some(head.key.clone());

   241	        // continues from there; with a lane that examined nothing, it ends.
   242	        let frontier = cut_frontier.unwrap_or_else(|| Self::frontier_index(&lanes, order));

   246	    fn open_branch_lane<'a>(&mut self, lane: &'a MergeLane, prefix: Vec<Value>, after: Option<&(Vec<Value>, bool)>, grant: Option<super::super::expiry::Pending>, metered: bool) -> Result<Lane<'a>, RuntimeError> {
   247	        let scan = &lane.scan;
   248	        let definition = &self.schema.tables[&scan.table]; let index = &definition.indexes[&scan.index];
   249	        // A branch cursor was validated once above. Do not clone the compiled
   250	        // Scan merely to clear it; evaluate only the remaining bounds here.
   251	        let mut bounds = self.word_bounds(scan, definition, index, None, metered, true)?;
   252	        if metered && let Some((values, _)) = after { self.meter.heap(DisclosedKeys(values).heap())?; }
   253	        let point_id = rules::membership_point_id(self.schema, lane);
   254	        let mut done = false;
   255	        if point_id {
   256	            // The point's pinned id is also its logical page position. Decide
   257	            // the shared cursor here instead of applying it after the fully
   258	            // consumed physical prefix.
   259	            if let Some((values, inclusive)) = after {
   260	                let position = &prefix[prefix.len() - 1..];
   261	                let comparison = Self::compare(position, values, scan.order);
   262	                done = comparison == Ordering::Less || comparison == Ordering::Equal && !inclusive;
   263	            }
   264	        } else {
   265	            match after { Some((values, true)) => bounds.at_or_after = Some(values.clone()), Some((values, false)) => bounds.after = Some(values.clone()), None => {} }
   266	        }
   267	        let suffix = index.components.iter().skip(scan.prefix.len()).filter_map(|component| component.key_name());
   268	        let (position_len, ends_in_id) = suffix.fold((0usize, false), |(len, _), name| (len + 1, name == "id"));
   269	        let position_len = position_len + usize::from(!ends_in_id || point_id);
   270	        Ok(Lane { scan, prefix, bounds, buffer: VecDeque::new(), done, grant, vacancy: lane.vacancy(), metered, position_len, through: None })
   271	    }

   273	    /// Read a lane's next batch strictly after its position, recording how
   274	    /// far it examined.
   275	    fn fill_branch(&mut self, lane: &mut Lane<'_>, batch: usize) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   276	        if lane.done { return Ok(()); }
   277	        // Arm the capture with the lane's own table and index: `scan_consumed`
   278	        // then records the last rule-visible candidate this read examined,
   279	        // filter rejects included (never a nested read's key).
   280	        if lane.metered { self.meter.heap(word_heap::text(&lane.scan.table) + word_heap::text(&lane.scan.index))?; }
   281	        let prior = self.scan_frontier.replace((lane.scan.table.clone(), lane.scan.index.clone(), None));
   282	        let rows = self.read_branch(lane, batch);
   283	        let examined = std::mem::replace(&mut self.scan_frontier, prior).and_then(|(_, _, key)| key);
   284	        let rows = rows?;
   285	        let capped = self.read_capped();
   286	        lane.done = rows.len() < batch && !capped;
   287	        let mut read_to = None;
   288	        if capped && let Some(key) = examined {
   289	            if lane.metered { self.meter.heap(word_heap::key_decode(lane.prefix.len() + lane.position_len, key.len()))?; }
   290	            if let Some(mut values) = crate::key::decode(&key) {
   291	                if lane.prefix.len() <= values.len() { values.drain(..lane.prefix.len()); }
   292	                values.truncate(lane.position_len);
   293	                if values.len() == lane.position_len { read_to = Some(values); }
   294	            }
   295	        }
   296	        if read_to.is_none() && let Some(row) = rows.last() {
   297	            self.charge_branch_position(lane, row)?;
   298	            read_to = Some(self.branch_position(lane, row));
   299	        }
   300	        if read_to.is_some() { lane.through = read_to; }
   301	        lane.buffer = rows.into();
   302	        Ok(())
   303	    }
   304	
   305	    /// The position every unfinished lane has examined through: the least in
   306	    /// the page's order, or `None` when some lane examined nothing past the
   307	    /// cursor. A finished lane bounds nothing.
   308	    fn frontier_index(lanes: &[Lane<'_>], order: Order) -> Option<usize> {
   309	        let mut least: Option<usize> = None;
   310	        for (position, lane) in lanes.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_, lane)| !lane.done) {
   311	            let through = lane.through.as_ref()?;
   312	            if least.is_none_or(|n| Self::compare(through, lanes[n].through.as_ref().expect("frontier"), order) == Ordering::Less) { least = Some(position); }
   313	        }
   314	        least
   315	    }

   317	    fn compare(a: &[Value], b: &[Value], order: Order) -> Ordering {
   318	        let ordering = a.iter().zip(b).find_map(|(x, y)| match x.total_cmp(y) { Ordering::Equal => None, other => Some(other) }).unwrap_or(a.len().cmp(&b.len()));
   319	        if order == Order::Asc { ordering } else { ordering.reverse() }
   320	    }
   321	
   322	    /// `key` is at or before `through` in the page's order.
   323	    fn within(key: &[Value], through: Option<&Vec<Value>>, order: Order) -> bool {
   324	        through.is_some_and(|through| Self::compare(key, through, order) != Ordering::Greater)
   325	    }
   326	
   327	    /// One read of a lane strictly after its current position. A lane its
   328	    /// grant admitted reads as a decided page (`scan_consumed`'s grant arm).
   329	    fn read_branch(&mut self, lane: &Lane<'_>, batch: usize) -> Result<Vec<Value>, RuntimeError> {
   330	        self.merge_grant = lane.grant;
   331	        self.merge_vacancy = lane.vacancy;
   332	        if lane.metered { self.meter.heap(DisclosedKeys(&lane.prefix).heap() + DisclosedBounds(&lane.bounds).heap())?; }
   333	        let result = self.scan_consumed(&lane.scan, lane.prefix.clone(), lane.bounds.clone(), batch, None, None, false, lane.metered);
   334	        self.merge_grant = None;
   335	        self.merge_vacancy = false;
   336	        Ok(result?.0)
   337	    }
   338	
   339	    fn branch_prefix_values(&mut self, scan: &Scan, metered: bool) -> Result<Vec<Value>, RuntimeError> {
   340	        if metered { self.meter.heap(word_heap::slots::<Value>(scan.prefix.len()))?; }
   341	        let mut values = Vec::with_capacity(scan.prefix.len());
   342	        for key in &scan.prefix {
   343	            values.push(match key {
   344	                IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(expr) => self.expr(expr)?,
   345	                IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(_) => return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("a branch prefix is never a words prefix".into())),
   346	            });
   347	        }
   348	        Ok(values)
   349	    }
   350	
   351	    fn clone_branch_prefix(&mut self, prefix: &[Value], metered: bool) -> Result<Vec<Value>, RuntimeError> {
   352	        if metered { self.meter.heap(DisclosedKeys(prefix).heap())?; }
   353	        Ok(prefix.to_vec())
   354	    }
   355	
   356	    fn push_branch_lane<'a>(&mut self, lanes: &mut Vec<Lane<'a>>, lane: Lane<'a>, metered: bool, site: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   357	        if metered {
   358	            self.meter.at(site);
   359	            if !self.read_charge(MeterKind::Steps, 1)? { return Ok(()); }
   360	            word_heap::push(&mut self.meter, lanes, lane)
   361	        } else { lanes.push(lane); Ok(()) }
   362	    }
   363	
   364	    fn push_branch_head(meter: &mut crate::meter::Meter, heap: &mut BinaryHeap<MergeHead>, head: MergeHead, metered: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   365	        if metered {
   366	            let additional = word_heap::grow::<MergeHead>(meter, heap.len(), heap.capacity())?;
   367	            if additional != 0 { heap.reserve_exact(additional); }
   368	        }
   369	        heap.push(head);
   370	        Ok(())
   371	    }
   372	
   373	    fn charge_branch_position(&mut self, lane: &Lane<'_>, row: &Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   374	        if !lane.metered { return Ok(()); }
   375	        let index = &self.schema.tables[&lane.scan.table].indexes[&lane.scan.index];
   376	        let copied = index.components.iter().skip(lane.prefix.len()).filter_map(|part| part.column())
   377	            .filter_map(|name| row.field(name).ok()).map(|value| DisclosedKey(value).copy_heap()).sum::<u64>();
   378	        let appended = if index.components.len() - lane.prefix.len() < lane.position_len {
   379	            row.field("id").map_or(0, |value| DisclosedKey(value).copy_heap())
   380	        } else { 0 };

    26	        let tail: Vec<_> = line.chars().skip(last.site.col as usize - 1).collect();
    27	        let mut length = last.value.chars().count();
    28	        if last.kind == Kind::Str {
    29	            length = 1;
    30	            while length < tail.len() {
    31	                let c = tail[length];
    32	                length += 1;
    33	                if c == tail[0] { break; }
    34	                if c == '\\' { length += 1; }
    35	            }
    36	        }
    37	        let mut text = String::new();
    38	        for number in first.site.line..=last.site.line {
    39	            let Some(line) = lines.get(number as usize - 1) else { return };
    40	            if number != first.site.line { text.push('\n'); }
    41	            let start = if number == first.site.line { first.site.col as usize - 1 } else { 0 };
    42	            let end = if number == last.site.line { last.site.col as usize - 1 + length } else { line.chars().count() };
    43	            text.extend(line.chars().skip(start).take(end.saturating_sub(start)));
    44	        }
    45	        let mut depth = 0_i32;
    46	        let grouped = first.kind == Kind::Symbol && first.value == "(" && tokens.iter().enumerate().all(|(i, t)| {
    47	            if t.kind == Kind::Symbol {
    48	                if t.value == "(" { depth += 1; }
    49	                if t.value == ")" { depth -= 1; }
    50	            }
    51	            depth > 0 || i + 1 == tokens.len()
    52	        }) && depth == 0;
    53	        self.expressions.insert(expr.site().to_string(), (expr.clone(), text, grouped, first.site.clone()));
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    pub(crate) fn get(&self, expr: &Expr) -> Option<(String, bool)> {
    57	        let (syntax, text, grouped, _) = self.expressions.get(&expr.site().to_string())?;
    58	        // A planner may reuse a site while changing the expression.
    59	        (syntax == expr).then(|| (text.clone(), *grouped))
    60	    }
    61	
    62	    pub(crate) fn read(&self, site: &crate::Site) -> Option<(crate::ast::ReadExpr, String, bool, crate::Site)> {
    63	        let (expr, text, grouped, start) = self.expressions.get(&site.to_string())?;
    64	        if let Expr::Read(read) = expr { return Some((read.clone(), text.clone(), *grouped, start.clone())); }
    65	        // Keyed write diagnostics share the read repair verifier. Replace only
    66	        // the target; keep the write kind, condition and payload in its caller.
    67	        let Expr::Write { table, keys, site, .. } = expr else { return None; };
    68	        let tokens = crate::lexer::lex("", text).0;
    69	        let table_token = tokens.get(1)?;
    70	        let mut depth = 0;
    71	        let close = tokens.iter().skip(2).find(|token| {
    72	            if token.kind != Kind::Symbol { return false; }
    73	            match token.value.as_str() { "[" => depth += 1, "]" => { depth -= 1; if depth == 0 { return true; } }, _ => {} }
    74	            false
    75	        })?;
    76	        let offset = |site: &crate::Site| {
    77	            let lines = text.split_inclusive('\n').collect::<Vec<_>>();
    78	            let line = site.line.checked_sub(1)? as usize;
    79	            Some(lines.get(..line)?.iter().map(|s| s.len()).sum::<usize>() + lines.get(line)?.char_indices()
    80	                .map(|(i, _)| i).nth(site.col.checked_sub(1)? as usize)?)
    81	        };
    82	        let from = offset(&table_token.site)?;
    83	        let end = offset(&close.site)? + 1;
    84	        let mut target_site = start.clone();
    85	        target_site.line += table_token.site.line - 1;
    86	        target_site.col = if table_token.site.line == 1 { start.col + table_token.site.col - 1 } else { table_token.site.col };
    87	        Some((crate::ast::ReadExpr { table: table.clone(), keys: keys.clone(), site: site.clone(), index: None,
    88	            cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), order_keys: Vec::new(), range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new() },
    89	            text[from..end].into(), false, target_site))
    90	    }
    91	
    92	    pub(crate) fn expression(&self, expr: &Expr) -> String {
    93	        crate::print::with_source(expr, &|expr| self.get(expr))
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    /// A comparison's operand needs parentheses around booleans and conditionals.
    97	    pub(crate) fn operand(&self, expr: &Expr) -> String {
    98	        crate::print::with_source_at(expr, 5, &|expr| self.get(expr))
    99	    }
   100	}
   101	
   102	#[cfg(test)]
   103	mod tests {
   104	    #[test]
   105	    fn explicit_index_search_sites_retain_authored_spelling() {
   106	        let bound = crate::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "table posts:\n  body: text <=40\n  at: time\n  by byWords: words(body), at, id\n  public\n  sync public\nquery posts(q: text <=40):\n  return posts matching q first 10 by byWords\n".into())]);
   107	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
   108	        let sites = &bound.schema.operations["posts"].sites;
   109	        assert_eq!(sites.len(), 2, "search and browse alternatives");
   110	        assert!(sites.iter().all(|site| site.site == "schema.q:8:10 (posts matching q first 10 by byWords)"), "{sites:?}");
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    #[test]
   114	    fn canonical_backend_sites_do_not_replace_authored_diagnostic_spelling() {
   115	        let bound = crate::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "table posts:\n  public\n  sync public\nquery posts():\n  return posts first 10\n".into())]);
   116	        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
   117	        let program = &bound.programs[0];
   118	        assert_eq!(program.sources["programs.posts.0"].1, "posts first 10");
   119	        assert_eq!(bound.schema.operations["posts"].sites[0].site, "schema.q:5:10 (posts[] first 10)");
   120	    }
   121	}

  1081	
  1082	**Static grants and whole-group reads.** An owner-or-readable-static-grant
  1083	rule uses the existing party/grant merge on synced union tables too. The
  1084	server evaluates the positive readable grant once; its holder reads the

     1	//! The Lean model's case format (LLP 3000.035 §5.2): a checked Quarry
     2	//! program, translated from its **surface** syntax tree into the terms of
     3	//! `snapback4/lean/Quarry/Syntax.lean`, as the JSON that
     4	//! `snapback4/lean/Quarry/CaseJson.lean` decodes (format:
     5	//! `snapback4/lean/README.md`, "The case format").
     6	//!
     7	//! **Why the surface tree.** The model is a second implementation of the
     8	//! binder and the interpreter together. Reading the binder's IR would copy
     9	//! the binder's decisions into the model's input, and the comparison could
    10	//! no longer find a binder defect. So nothing here reads `bind` or the IR:
    11	//! the input is the parsed [`Document`]s, and the only binder fact used is
    12	//! that [`crate::compile`] reported no diagnostics, i.e. that the program
    13	//! type-checks ([`lean`] refuses otherwise).
    14	//!
    15	//! **Types.** The few places that need a type (a text literal beside an id is
    16	//! an id, OPEN V1; a positional key chooses a unique key by its argument
    17	//! types, indexes card) take it from the surface's own declarations, which
    18	//! the type checker has accepted: column types, argument types, helper
    19	//! parameters, `viewer : principal`, and literal kinds, propagated through
    20	//! locals by the small inference in [`Scope::ty`]. When that inference cannot
    21	//! decide, the program is unsupported, never guessed.
    22	//!
    23	//! **The expansions** are those `Syntax.lean` documents: `public` is
    24	//! `read <- allow`; an omitted rule is `deny`; an unnamed `update <- R` is
    25	//! `R[old] and R[next]` (OPEN R2); positional keys become named equalities
    26	//! on `id` (an `id`-typed or `t`-typed single key) or on the one declared
    27	//! unique key whose column types accept the arguments in order; in a
    28	//! `where`, each `.col = e` conjunct (e not reading the row) is a pin and the
    29	//! remaining conjuncts are the filter; `return insert …` is a bound write
    30	//! followed by `return`.
    31	//!
    32	//! **Erased, with the reason.** `sync …` and `online only` (residency
    33	//! decides what a device holds, never a server outcome) and non-unique
    34	//! `by` indexes (tuning; reads choose indexes themselves). A `text <= N`
    35	//! bound is not modelled as a value restriction (OPEN W6):
    36	//! [`LeanModule::store`] and [`LeanModule::tag_args`] refuse inputs that exceed
    37	//! it, and a literal written past it is unsupported. The surface emitter does
    38	//! use the bound to keep PAGE cases with nonportable synthesized keys out of
    39	//! the fragment before the oracle. A computed text written to a bounded column
    40	//! is not checked.
    41	//!
    42	//! **Everything else** outside the model's core fragment is an
    43	//! [`Unsupported`] with a stable `reason` (counted by the harness, never a
    44	//! pass) and a `detail` naming the construct. A translation is all or
    45	//! nothing: there is no partial program.

    52	/// A construct outside the model's core fragment.

    55	    /// Stable, low-cardinality name (the harness counts by it).
   280	fn table_shape(t: &ast::TableDecl) -> R<Table> {
   281	    for (present, what) in [
   282	        (t.receipts.is_some(), "receipts"),
   283	        (t.ephemeral.is_some(), "ephemeral"),
   284	        (!t.search.is_empty(), "search"),

   334	fn table_json(t: &ast::TableDecl, shape: &Table, tables: &[Table]) -> R<Json> {

   873	    fn domain(&self, read: &ReadExpr, keyed: bool) -> R<(Vec<Json>, Json)> {

   885	        };

   891	        }

   896	                        (Some(col), None) if !reads_row(right) => Some((col, right)),

   905	                }
   906	            }
   907	            residual.push(focal.expr(c)?);
